TL;DR: 本文研究解码量子干涉(DQI)在内存资源上的量子优势，在流式模型下给出无条件可证的优势。作者考虑OPI的推广（基于Hermite插值与Hasse导数），证明量子算法能以单遍、多对数空间、每项多对数时间满足93%约束，而任何满足76%约束的经典算法即使多项式次遍历、无限时间也需多项式空间，从而给出完整的权衡曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI已被证明在时间维度上对最优多项式交集(OPI)具有量子优势，但空间（内存）资源同样关键：海量输入的存储与操作困难，尤其逻辑量子比特带有显著的容错实现开销。因此核心问题是：DQI是否带来内存层面的量子优势，且能否无条件证明。

Method: 在流式（streaming）设定下研究该问题，采用Hermite插值与Hasse导数对OPI做自然推广：要求在多项式的取值与导数上尽可能满足更多约束。在此基础上，将DQI算法改造为单遍流式算法，并分析其空间与每项时间复杂度；同时建立经典算法的空间下界。

Result: 量子效率：DQI的改造算法产出的多项式满足93%的约束，且仅单遍读取输入流，空间为多对数级，每个流项的计算时间为多对数级。经典硬度：任何经典算法若想满足76%的约束，即使允许多项式次遍历且时间不受限，仍需多项式空间。

Conclusion: 结果给出了可调参数下完整的权衡曲线，并蕴含DQI对原始OPI问题具有可证明的量子优势，即在内存资源上实现了无条件认证的量子优势。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) is a polynomial-time quantum algorithm introduced by Jordan et al. (Nature 2025). For a natural optimization problem, known as optimal polynomial intersection (OPI), it achieves approximation guarantees in regimes where all known classical algorithms require exponential time.
  Besides time, space is another central resource: storing and manipulating a massive input can be very challenging, especially when logical qubits carry substantial fault-tolerant implementation overhead. This motivates the following question: does DQI yield quantum advantages in memory, and can we prove it unconditionally?
  We give an affirmative answer to this question in the streaming setting. In particular, we consider a natural generalization of OPI using Hermite interpolation and Hasse derivatives, which asks for a low-degree polynomial satisfying as many constraints on its values and derivatives as possible. As a concrete example, we show
  [Quantum efficiency.] An adaptation of the DQI algorithm produces a polynomial satisfying $93\%$ of the constraints; moreover, it only reads the input stream in one pass, uses polylogarithmic space, and has polylogarithmic computation time per stream entry.
  [Classical hardness.] Any classical algorithm that produces an answer satisfying just $76\%$ of the constraints requires polynomial space, even if it can read the input stream with polynomially many passes and can use unlimited time.
  Our result provides a complete tradeoff curve for the tunable parameters, and implies that DQI has provable quantum advantages for the original OPI problem.

</details>


### [100] [Loss-tolerant distributed lattice surgery using fusion networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01923)
*Felix Burt,Richard Meister,Sheng-Ku Lin,Kuan-Cheng Chen,Michael Hanks,Roberto Bondesan,M. S. Kim,Kin K. Leung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 使用ZX演算变换构造混合症状提取协议，应用于分布式旋转表面码格点手术，实现接口擦除阈值50%，并将合并可观测量接口距离从d+1提升至2d+1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化物质量子处理单元需跨光子链路执行分布式逻辑操作，但光子损耗和概率性线性光学操作导致接口噪声强于本地。测量基与融合基量子计算对损耗和概率操作具有鲁棒性，有望在联网QPU接口处补充电路基纠错。

Method: 利用ZX演算变换构造混合症状提取协议，应用于分布式旋转表面码格点手术；分析截断资源态、资源态错误、本地电路噪声与融合擦除下的可纠正区域，并将擦除阈值转换为光子损耗阈值，采用融合增强探测亚阈值性能。

Result: 在无本地噪声时，多种协议达到50%接口擦除阈值，包括使用融合Bell对的电路基格点手术和基于线性簇态的混合协议；相对直接Bell对接口几何，混合协议将合并可观测量接口距离从d+1增至2d+1，并将垂直可观测量接口距离从⌊(d+1)/2⌋恢复至d。在电路与资源态错误率10^-3时本地错误基本掩盖接口距离优势；降至10^-4及以下时线性链协议随擦除降低改善更快。

Conclusion: 接口距离增强在低本地噪声区域有效，混合融合基协议可提升联网QPU接口容错性能，尤其适用于本地噪声被充分抑制的场景。

Abstract: Networking matter-based quantum processing units (QPUs) offers a promising route to scaling fault-tolerant quantum computers. This requires distributed logical operations to be performed across photonic links, where noise is characteristically different from and stronger than in local QPUs owing to photon loss and probabilistic linear-optical operations. Measurement- and fusion-based quantum computing are designed to be robust against loss and probabilistic photonic operations, suggesting they could complement circuit-based error correction at the interface between networked QPUs. We use ZX calculus transformations to construct hybrid syndrome extraction protocols and apply them to distributed rotated surface code lattice surgery, demonstrating that several protocols attain a $50\%$ interface-erasure threshold when local noise is absent, including circuit-based lattice surgery using fused Bell pairs and hybrid protocols using linear cluster states. Relative to a straight Bell-pair interface geometry, the hybrid protocols increase the merge-observable interface distance from $d+1$ to $2d+1$ and restore the perpendicular-observable interface distance from $\lfloor(d+1)/2\rfloor$ to $d$. We calculate how the threshold decreases when using truncated resource states and map correctable regions under resource-state errors, local circuit noise, and fusion erasure. We then convert these erasure thresholds into photon-loss thresholds and probe subthreshold performance using fusion boosting. At circuit and resource-state error rates of $10^{-3}$, local errors largely mask the interface-distance advantage. As local noise is reduced to $10^{-4}$ and below, the linear-chain protocols improve more rapidly with decreasing erasure, suggesting that interface distance enhancements are effective in low local noise regimes.

</details>


### [101] [Avoiding Exponentially Large Groups with Open Quantum System Technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01932)
*Jihong Cai,Advith Govindarajan,Marius Junge*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于开放量子系统的态制备新方法：放弃在 $n$ 个量子比特上使用整个酉群，转而构造 $(n+3)$ 个量子比特上的小 Lie 群 $G$（其 Lie 代数维数为 $\mathrm{poly}(n)$），通过反复结合部分迹、环境态制备与群操作，可从任意固定输入态逼近任意量子态，并进一步实现密度矩阵空间的传递性与量子信道的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统态制备与量子信道实现通常依赖 $n$ 量子比特上的完整酉群，其参数规模随比特数指数增长，导致控制代价高昂。作者希望利用开放量子系统的自由度，寻找参数量仅为多项式级的小群结构，从而以更低成本实现态与信道的普适制备。

Method: 核心方法是在 $(n+3)$ 量子比特上识别一个维数为 $\mathrm{poly}(n)$ 的小 Lie 群 $G$，该群由局域 Pauli 串的 Lie 代数导出；通过交替使用部分迹、环境侧的态制备以及群操作，从任意固定输入态逼近任意量子态。此外，作者识别出一个单一的开放系统“magic”相互作用哈密顿量，其酉群与上述开放系统技术结合后可在所有密度矩阵空间上实现传递作用；对于 $n$ 量子比特上的信道，则借助更大的环境实现信道普适性。

Result: 所构造的小 Lie 群维数仅为 $\mathrm{poly}(n)$；结合部分迹与环境态制备，可从任意固定输入态逼近任意量子态；单一相互作用哈密顿量配合开放系统技术可在密度矩阵空间上实现传递性；使用更大环境可实现 $n$ 量子比特信道的普适性。这些群源自局域 Pauli 串 Lie 代数，并由此引出一套关于“廉价态、密度与信道”和“昂贵态、密度与信道”的新分类框架。

Conclusion: 通过开放量子系统与由局域 Pauli 串导出的小 Lie 群，可在多项式资源下实现态制备与信道的普适性，突破对完整酉群的依赖，并为量子态、密度矩阵与信道的资源代价提供新的分层视角。

Abstract: We propose a novel approach to state preparation by embracing the full power of open quantum systems. Instead of working with the whole unitary group in $n$ qubits, we identify a small Lie group $G$ in $(n+3)$ qubits. The dimension of its Lie algebra is $\text{poly}(n)$. Every state can be approximated starting from any fixed input state by repeatedly using partial trace and state preparation in the environment alongside group operations. Moreover, we also identify a single (open system magic) interaction Hamiltonian whose unitary group can be combined with open system quantum technology mentioned above to achieve transitivity on the space of all densities. Similarly, we show that all channels on $n$ qubits can be approximated by using a larger environment, achieving channel universality. The interesting small groups we identify are derived from Lie algebras of local Pauli strings and lead to a new landscape of cheap and expensive states, densities, and channels.

</details>


### [102] [Optimal query complexity for fractional quantum evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01940)
*Anthony Yuezhang Liu,Adam Wesołowski,Jayne Thompson,Mile Gu,Lirandë Pira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文解决了分数查询问题的查询复杂度下界，证明QSVT上界渐近最优，最优复杂度为Θ_τ((1/δ)log(1/ε))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数查询问题：给定未知酉算子的oracle访问U=e^{iH}，在谱与分支割线间隔为δ时，实现非整数幂U^t=e^{itH} (0<t<1) 需要多少查询。已知QSVT给出O((1/δ)log(1/ε))查询的上界，但缺乏匹配的下界，无法判定其是否最优。

Method: 将任意N次查询电路归约为用次数被O(N)界定的三角多项式逼近e^{itθ}，并结合Remez不等式建立下界。另给出基于构造一个湮灭逼近空间的线性泛函的替代下界证明。

Result: 证明了对任意查询算法的匹配下界Ω_τ((1/δ)log(1/ε))；替代证明给出对δ一致的Ω_τ(log(1/ε))下界。

Conclusion: 分数查询问题的最优查询复杂度为Θ_τ((1/δ)log(1/ε))，表明已知的QSVT构造渐近最优。

Abstract: Given oracle access to an unknown unitary $U=e^{iH}$ , the fractional query problem asks how many queries are required to implement a noninteger power $U^t=e^{itH}$, $0<t<1$, when the spectrum is separated from the branch cut by a gap $δ$. Quantum singular value transformation gives an upper bound of $O\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$ queries for approximation error $\varepsilon$. We prove a matching lower bound for arbitrary query algorithms. Our argument reduces any $N$-query circuit to the approximation of $e^{itθ}$ by a trigonometric polynomial with degree bounded by $O(N)$, together with Remez inequality. This allows us to establish the lower bound of $Ω_τ\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$. Consequently, the optimal query complexity for fractional query problem is $Θ_τ\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$, showing that the known QSVT construction is asymptotically optimal. We also give an alternative lower bound proof based on constructing a linear functional that annihilates the approximant space, yielding a $Ω_τ\!\left(\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$ bound uniform to $δ$.

</details>


### [103] [Universality Sacrifices Reliability in Classical-Quantum Channel Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01941)
*Kaito Watanabe,Masahito Hayashi,Takaya Matsuura,Hao-Chung Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 经典信道中通用编码可同时达到容量与最优高码率可靠性，但本文证明经典-量子信道中该兼容性一般不成立。作者刻画了通用编码下最优可靠性的特征，指出信道感知与通用误差指数分别由Petz与sandwiched Rényi散度支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典信道中，通用编码无需完整信道描述即可同时实现容量与最优高码率可靠性；作者希望考察这一良好性质能否推广到经典-量子信道，特别是当输出系统存在未知酉旋转时通用编码是否仍能达到信道感知下的最优可靠性。

Method: 构造一族经典-量子信道，证明不存在固定编码方案可达到信道感知最优可靠性；针对酉不变解码器推导可靠性反界，并构造与信道无关的编解码器对，给出通用可达可靠性界；用Petz散度与sandwiched Rényi散度分别刻画信道感知与通用误差指数，比较高码率渐近行为。

Result: 在高码率区域，通用可达可靠性界与酉不变解码器的反界相匹配，从而刻画了最优通用可靠性；信道感知与通用误差指数分别由Petz散度和sandwiched Rényi散度支配，二者在输出可交换时一致、在非交换时不同，解释了经典情形通用性保持最优可靠性而量子情形可能降低可靠性的原因。

Conclusion: 经典-量子信道中通用编码无法普遍达到信道感知最优可靠性；酉不变解码器下的最优通用可靠性由sandwiched Rényi散度决定。该结果揭示了通用地进行经典-量子信道编码任务所固有的基本可靠性代价，并给出经典与量子情形差异的散度根源。

Abstract: Universal channel coding enables communication without a complete description of the channel. For classical channels, universal codes can attain both capacity and the optimal high-rate reliability. We show that this compatibility fails for classical-quantum channels in general; that is, the optimal reliability in the channel-aware scenario is not always achievable with universal coding due to the ignorance of the unitary rotation of the output system. We exhibit a family of classical-quantum channels for which one cannot achieve the channel-aware optimal reliability by a fixed coding scheme. We further derive a converse bound on the reliability for unitary-invariant decoders, a natural assumption for the universal coding scheme, that can be strictly smaller than the optimal channel-aware error exponent. Conversely, we construct a channel-independent encoder-decoder pair and establish a universally achievable bound on the reliability that matches this converse bound in the high-rate regime, thereby characterizing the optimal universal reliability. Specifically, the channel-aware and universal exponents are governed by the Petz and sandwiched Rényi divergences, respectively. These divergences coincide for commuting outputs but differ for noncommuting ones, explaining why universality preserves optimal reliability classically but can reduce it quantumly. Our results showcase the fundamental reliability cost of performing the classical-quantum channel coding task universally.

</details>


### [104] [Hardware-Efficient Ground-State Preparation using Variational Imaginary-Time Majorana Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01954)
*Federico Santona,Manuel G. Algaba,Aeishah Ameera Anuar,Anna M. Wernbacher,Prachi Sharma,Fedor Šimkovic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出VIME经典预训练算法并结合c-tUPS压缩拟设，实现资源高效的分子基态制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期容错量子化学需要紧凑的基态制备电路，而强关联与多参考体系下现有变分方法（如ADAPT-VMPE）电路资源开销大、精度受限。

Method: 将算子投影变分量子虚时演化（OVQITE）扩展到分子电子结构，构建单一编译的Majorana传播（MP）代理图形成VIME；并设计压缩分块幺正乘积态（c-tUPS）拟设以降低经典模拟与量子态制备成本。

Result: 在强关联钌配合物TLD-1411（最多52量子比特）中达到化学精度，能量误差较ADAPT-VMPE小约400倍；在至七并苯（60量子比特）的强多参考并苯系列中，精度持平或更优，CNOT数最多减少26倍，双量子比特深度降低167倍。

Conclusion: 虚时经典预训练压缩变分拟设是通向资源高效分子基态制备的有效途径。

Abstract: Compact ground-state preparation circuits are essential for early fault-tolerant quantum chemistry. We introduce Variational Imaginary-time Majorana Evolution (VIME), a classical pre-training algorithm that extends operator-projected variational quantum imaginary-time evolution (OVQITE) to molecular electronic structure using a single compiled Majorana-propagation (MP) surrogate graph. We pair VIME with a compressed tiled Unitary Product State (c-tUPS) ansatz that we develop to reduce both classical simulation and quantum state-preparation costs. In the strongly correlated ruthenium complex TLD-1411, active-space calculations requiring up to 52 qubits achieve chemical precision against DMRG reference energies, with energy errors approximately $400\times$ smaller than those from ADAPT-VQE-based variational Majorana-propagation (ADAPT-VMPE). Across the strongly multireference acene series up to heptacene (60 qubits), VIME matches or improves on the MP-reported energy accuracy of the ADAPT-VMPE comparison points with up to $26\times$ fewer CNOTs and $167\times$ lower two-qubit depth. These results establish imaginary-time classical pre-training of compressed variational ansätze as a promising route to resource-efficient molecular ground-state preparation.

</details>


### [105] [Fully tolerant product state testing and closest product state learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01979)
*Zongbo Bao,Jonas Helsen,Tuyen Nguyen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究未知n-qudit态ρ与乘积态的接近性检验问题，提出时间高效且所需副本数与n无关的算法，并给出改进的最接近乘积态学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断未知量子态是否接近或远离乘积态是量子态检验的重要问题，同时学习最接近的乘积态也具有基础意义；已有方法在副本数或效率上存在不足，尤其希望消除对系统规模n的依赖并获得高效容错检验与学习算法。

Method: 采用随机着色论证：对任意未知态，总存在将[n]划分为q部分的划分，使与该划分下最近乘积态的overlap平方仅增加O(1/q)的可加因子，从而把问题归约为q方（局部维度可能增长）的容错检验；结合分块谱投影论证，证明Harrow & Montanaro乘积态检验的自然k-copy推广是高效容错检验器。学习方面同样使用随机着色与分块谱投影，并引入qudit版Bakshi等人的高保真乘积态学习算法及基于Werner最优克隆信道的采样技术。

Result: 检验算法时间高效且所需未知态副本数与n无关；k-copy推广的乘积态检验为高效容错检验器。学习算法使用\widetilde{O}((nd)^2) 2^{\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^8)}个副本，输出\varepsilon-近似最优乘积态。

Conclusion: 随机着色与分块谱投影是统一且有效的技术工具，可用于乘积态容错检验和最接近乘积态学习，并显著改进学习算法的副本复杂度。

Abstract: We address the problem of testing whether an unknown $n$-qudit state $ρ$ is $a$-close to a product state or $b$-far away from any product state, as measured in terms of state overlap. We provide a time-efficient algorithm to solve this problem that requires an $n$-independent number of copies of the unknown state. Our random coloring argument shows that for any unknown state there always exists a partition of $[n]$ into $q$ parts such that the square of the overlap with the closest product state with respect to this partition is only an additive factor $O(1/q)$ larger. This reduces the problem to tolerant testing of $q$ parties, with potentially growing local dimensions. Combining this insight with blockwise spectral projection arguments we can show that the natural $k$-copy generalization of Harrow & Montanaro's product state test provides an efficient tolerant tester.
  We use the same random coloring and blockwise spectral projection techniques to obtain a substantially improved algorithm for closest product state learning. In particular we give an algorithm that takes in $\widetilde{O}\big((nd)^2\big)\, 2^{\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^8)}$ copies of the unknown state and produces an $\varepsilon$-approximately optimal product state. The key technical components of this learner are a qudit variant of the Bakshi et al. high-fidelity product state learning algorithm and a sampling technique based on Werner's optimal cloning channel.

</details>


### [106] [On the Metastability of the Mean-Field Interchange Model for Local Dimension $\geq 3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01987)
*Sergio Escobar,Lin Lin,Michael Ragone,Kevin D. Stubbs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究平均场互换模型在低温下的量子热化行为，证明其Davies动力学存在两个特征逆温度阈值，在静态一阶相变区间内谱隙随系统尺寸指数衰减（亚稳态），而区间外谱隙仅为多项式小量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多量子系统因亚稳相的存在而在低温下热化缓慢。作者关注是否存在进一步降温反而恢复多项式时间热化的情形，并以平均场互换模型的Davies动力学作为具体研究对象来揭示这一现象。

Method: 采用Davies生成元的谱隙分析，利用Schur-Weyl对偶将生成元分解为Young图上的经典马尔可夫生成元，并分析其余对称扇区（其谱隙为Ω(n^{-1})）；设定局部维度d≥3及单格点广义Pauli耦合。

Result: 系统存在两个特征逆温度β_min(d)与β=d，静态一阶相变位于二者之间。在区间(β_min(d), d)内谱隙随系统尺寸n指数消失，表明亚稳态；在该区间两侧（低温与高温）谱隙下界为系统尺寸的逆多项式，呈多项式时间热化。

Conclusion: 指数级慢化完全源于Young图链部分，其余对称扇区的谱隙为Ω(n^{-1})。该结果展示了一类可随降温恢复多项式时间热化的量子动力学，并给出其动力学相图。

Abstract: Many quantum systems are believed to thermalize slowly at low temperatures due to the existence of metastable phases. However, there are cases where further cooling can restore polynomial-time thermalization: we demonstrate that the Davies dynamics of the mean-field interchange model with local dimension $d\geq 3$ and single-site generalized Pauli couplings exhibits this behavior. The dynamical phase diagram is beholden to two distinguished inverse temperatures, $β_{\mathrm{min}}(d)$ and $β=d$, with the static first-order phase transition lying between them. At low and high temperatures surrounding the interval $(β_{\mathrm{min}}(d),d)$, the spectral gap of the Davies generator is bounded below by an inverse polynomial in system size $n$. Meanwhile, in this interval, the gap vanishes exponentially in $n$, indicating metastability. The proof uses Schur-Weyl duality to decompose the Davies generator into a classical Markov generator on Young diagrams, and the restriction to the remaining symmetry sectors whose gap is $Ω(n^{-1})$. Thus, exponential slowdown arises entirely from the Young diagram chain.

</details>


### [107] [Geometry of Knill-Laflamme Coefficients for Pauli Error Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01992)
*Baisong Sun,Ningping Cao,Yiu Tung Poon,Bei Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Knill-Laflamme系数的几何结构：在给定Pauli可观测量与码维数下，可达系数向量构成联合高阶数值域，其欧氏范数像为签名谱。主定理表明表示重数足够时可将活动期望数据提升为精确标量压缩，此时系数范围为满活动期望体，凸且连通，签名谱为闭区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Knill-Laflamme系数通过误差算子到码空间的标量压缩刻画精确量子纠错检测，但系数向量可达集合的几何形状如何受算子表示、码维数与检测约束影响尚缺乏统一结构框架。本文旨在刻画该几何，并厘清凸性、连通性与签名谱区间性的充分条件。

Method: 将给定Pauli可观测量与固定码维数下的可达系数向量刻画为联合高阶数值域，其欧氏范数像定义为签名谱。通过主定理给出"表示重数充分性"判据：当每个被占据块的重数至少为码维数乘以相应活动状态秩时，活动期望数据可提升为精确标量压缩。结合子系统稳定子码与母哈密顿量构造进行验证，并给出显式算例。

Result: 当重数足以实现所有活动状态时，系数范围等于满活动期望体，因而凸且连通，签名谱为闭区间。该框架统一了：交换Pauli族（充分公共本征空间简并给出多胞体）与子系统稳定子码（受保护逻辑子系统为非交换规范可观测量提供重数空间）。显例显示增大码维数可使范围收缩、坍缩或变空，而增大重数可将Bloch球变为实心球；子系统设置中母哈密顿量构造可将精确检测码实现为简并基态空间并选出连续路径。三量子比特例子表明额外非交换检测约束可将四面体范围约化为其重心与四个顶点，导致签名谱断连。

Conclusion: 本文为Knill-Laflamme系数几何提供了结构框架，指出重数是决定系数范围凸性、连通性与签名谱区间性的关键因素，并激励在结构化误差模型下寻求更精细的连通系数范围与区间签名谱判据。

Abstract: Knill-Laflamme coefficients characterize exact quantum error detection through scalar compressions of error operators to the code space. For prescribed Pauli observables and a fixed code dimension, attainable coefficient vectors form a joint higher-rank numerical range, whose Euclidean norm image is the signature spectrum. We study how operator representation, code dimension, and detection constraints govern this geometry. Our main theorem identifies representation multiplicity as sufficient to lift active expectation data to exact scalar compressions: a lift exists whenever each occupied block has multiplicity at least the code dimension times the rank of the corresponding active state. When multiplicity suffices to realize all active states, the coefficient range is the full active expectation body and is therefore convex and connected, with a closed interval as its signature spectrum. This framework unifies commuting Pauli families, where sufficient common-eigenspace degeneracy yields polytopes, and subsystem stabilizer codes, where protected logical subsystems supply multiplicity spaces for noncommuting gauge observables. Explicit examples show how increasing the code dimension can make a range shrink, collapse, or become empty, while increasing multiplicity can turn a Bloch sphere into a filled ball. In the subsystem setting, parent-Hamiltonian constructions realize exact detecting codes as degenerate ground spaces, selecting distinguished vectors and continuous paths within these ranges. A final three-qubit example shows additional noncommuting detection constraints reduce a tetrahedral range to its barycenter and four vertices, producing a disconnected signature spectrum. The results provide a structural framework for Knill-Laflamme coefficient geometry and motivate sharper criteria for connected coefficient ranges and interval signature spectra under structured error models.

</details>


### [108] [Operational transformation rates of quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02025)
*Giulia Mazzola,Renato Renner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文放松量子态多拷贝的iid假设，引入与张量幂态在操作上不可区分的资源态集合，用de Finetti型论证证明其渐近可由almost-iid态表示，并给出纠缠成本、蒸馏等渐近变换率的操作类比，进而追问其是否与iid对应量一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上用量子态的张量幂描述多拷贝，但实验者一般无法排除未知位置的不完美或拷贝间关联，因此假定独立同分布(iid)拷贝缺乏先验正当性。作者希望在更弱的假设下建立量子资源理论框架。

Method: 放松iid假设，考虑一整组与张量幂态在根本上不可区分（对实验者而言看起来像该量子态的多拷贝）的资源态；利用de Finetti型论证，证明该操作态集合渐近可表示为almost-iid态；并据此定义渐近变换率的操作类比，如纠缠成本与纠缠蒸馏。

Result: 证明该操作集合内的态渐近可由almost-iid态表示，从而为不依赖iid假设的资源理论提供基础；引入纠缠成本、蒸馏等渐近变换率的操作类比。

Conclusion: 在仅凭操作不可区分性定义的多拷贝资源态下，可渐近用almost-iid态刻画，并定义相应渐近变换率；核心开放问题是这些操作率是否与iid情形下的对应率相等。

Abstract: Typically, multiple copies of a quantum state are described by a tensor power of that state. However, from an operational point of view, an experimentalist cannot, in general, rule out imperfections at unknown positions or correlations between copies, so assuming independent and identically distributed (iid) copies is not justified a priori. In the following, we relax the iid assumption by considering a whole set of resource states that are fundamentally indistinguishable from a tensor power state, and in that sense look like many copies of the quantum state to the experimentalist. Using a de Finetti-type argument, we show that this operational set of states can asymptotically be represented by almost-iid states. As an application, we introduce operational analogues of asymptotic transformation rates, such as entanglement cost and distillation. This raises the question of whether these rates coincide with their iid counterparts.

</details>


### [109] [(A Variant of) Clifford Circuit Synthesis is NP-Complete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02029)
*Luna Lima Keller,Richard Kueng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究最优Clifford电路合成问题，证明其一个变体是NP-hard的，与已知的3-SAT启发式方法形成匹配的复杂度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优电路合成是量子与经典硬件设计的核心问题。经典版本已有成熟的启发式和严格的硬度结论，但最优量子电路合成的复杂度理解仍很欠缺。作者针对Clifford电路合成，填补其复杂度下界（硬度）方面的空白。

Method: 证明分两步：(i) 将3-正则图上的3-边着色问题归约到仅含CZ门的电路合成问题；(ii) 证明引入额外基本Clifford门（尤其是Hadamard、phase和CNOT）不会进一步降低最优电路深度。

Result: 证明了最优Clifford合成的一个变体是NP-hard的，从而与已有的3-SAT启发式方法形成匹配的复杂度结果。整体上厘清了Clifford电路的复杂度图景：仿真与等价性检查属于P，而判定某Clifford unitary能否在给定深度内实现则是NP-complete。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对Clifford电路复杂度理论的理解，确立了最优Clifford合成在给定深度判定问题上的NP-complete性质，为电路合成的硬度刻画提供了严格依据。

Abstract: Optimal circuit synthesis is the problem of finding the shortest-depth circuit representation of a given functionality with respect to a pre-specified elementary gate set. This is a central problem in both quantum and classical hardware design. While the classical version is very well understood -- both in terms of heuristics and rigorous hardness assertions -- much less is known about optimal quantum circuit synthesis.
  We focus on optimal Clifford circuit synthesis, for which various heuristics are known, e.g. via reduction to 3-SAT. Our main result supplies a matching hardness result: a variant of optimal Clifford synthesis is NP-hard. The proof proceeds in two parts: (i) reduce 3-edge colorability on 3-regular graphs to a circuit synthesis problem that only involves CZ gates, (ii) prove that the availability of additional elementary Clifford gates -- most notably: Hadamard, phase and CNOT -- cannot lead to further improvements of the optimal circuit depth. Our work sharpens the complexity-theoretic understanding of Clifford circuits: simulation and equivalence checking are in P, whereas deciding whether a Clifford unitary admits an implementation within a prescribed depth is NP-complete.

</details>


### [110] [Sparse Hamiltonian simulation with optimal dependence on the maximum column Euclidean norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02030)
*Zecheng Li,Chunhao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出模拟 d-稀疏 Hermitian 哈密顿量的量子算法，查询复杂度为 O(tΛ√d + √d·log(2/ε))，去除了 Low 算法的次多项式开销，在多数参数区间达到下界，并应用于稀疏量子线性系统与黑箱酉算符实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Low [STOC 2019] 的稀疏哈密顿量模拟算法存在次多项式（subpolynomial）开销，未达到已知最坏情况下界；同时 Berry 与 Childs [QIC 2012] 提出的黑箱酉算符实现的开问题尚未解决。本文旨在消除该开销并达到最优查询复杂度。

Method: 基于 d-稀疏 Hermitian 哈密顿量 H 及其最大列欧氏范数上界 Λ（或谱范数上界），通过稀疏 oracle 查询实现量子模拟，查询规模为 O(tΛ√d + √d·log(2/ε))；1-与 2-量子比特门数与查询规模成线性关系（至 oracle 代价及输入比特长度、对数精度参数的多项式开销）。

Result: 对 tΛ≥1/2，算子范数误差 ε 下使用 O(tΛ√d + √d·log(2/ε)) 次稀疏 oracle 查询；当 d>1 且 tΛ≥log(2/ε) 时达到最坏情况下界。应用方面：求解 d-稀疏量子线性系统（‖A‖≤1，‖A⁻¹‖≤κ）需 O(κ√d·polylog(κ/ε)) 次查询；每行每列至多 d 个非零元的黑箱酉算符实现需 O(√d·log(2/ε)) 次查询，常数误差下达到最优，对任意 N×N 酉算符给出 Θ(√N) 查询。

Conclusion: 该算法消除了 Low 算法中的次多项式开销，用加性对数精度项替代，并在广泛参数区间匹配最坏情况下界；同时解决了 Berry 与 Childs 关于黑箱酉算符实现的开放问题，给出门高效的实现方案。

Abstract: We give a quantum algorithm for simulating a $d$-sparse Hermitian Hamiltonian $H$, assuming a known upper bound $Λ$ on its maximum column Euclidean norm $\|H\|_{1\to2}$. For $tΛ\ge1/2$, simulation with operator-norm error $ε$ uses \[
  O\!\left(tΛ\sqrt d+\sqrt d\log(2/ε)\right) \] sparse-oracle queries. This removes the subpolynomial overhead in Low's algorithm [STOC 2019], replacing it with an additive logarithmic precision term. For $d>1$ and $tΛ\ge\log(2/ε)$, the bound matches the worst-case lower bound. A known spectral-norm upper bound may also be used in place of $Λ$. The number of 1- and 2-qubit gates is linear in the query scale, up to oracle costs and polynomial overhead in the input bit lengths and logarithmic precision parameters.
  As applications, we obtain $O(κ\sqrt d\,\mathrm{polylog}(κ/ε))$ queries for solving $d$-sparse quantum linear systems with $\|A\|\le1$ and $\|A^{-1}\|\leκ$, under standard sparse and state-preparation access. We also give a gate-efficient implementation of black-box unitaries with at most $d$ nonzero entries per row and column using $O(\sqrt d\log(2/ε))$ queries, given sparse access to the unitary and its adjoint. At constant error, the query bound is optimal and yields $Θ(\sqrt N)$ queries for arbitrary $N\times N$ unitaries, resolving the open question on black-box unitary implementation posed by Berry and Childs [QIC 2012].

</details>


### [111] [Adaptivity is all you need: Optimal stabilizer learning using just single-copy measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02031)
*L. Bittel,J. Eisert,W. Gong,A. A. Mele,L. Schatzki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明自适应单拷贝Clifford测量可以完全弥合稳定子态学习中的样本复杂度差距：仅用Θ(n)次单拷贝测量即可学习任意n量子比特稳定子态，达到Bell采样的最优样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子态在量子纠错、基准测试和经典模拟中至关重要，但其可学习性存在显著差距：两拷贝Bell测量只需Θ(n)个拷贝即可学习n量子比特稳定子态，而非自适应单拷贝测量却需要Ω(n^2)个拷贝。作者旨在探究自适应性能否弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出一种多项式时间自适应算法，使用Θ(n)次单拷贝Clifford测量学习任意n量子比特稳定子态。相同思想被扩展用于构造样本最优的单拷贝容错测试器，以及在具有k量子比特量子内存时达到最优测试权衡Θ(n-k+1/ε)。此外将该自适应机制推广至稳定子零度（stabilizer nullity）至多为r的态。

Result: 自适应单拷贝测量达到Θ(n)样本复杂度，与Bell采样的最优样本复杂度匹配，且无需任何多拷贝测量。在k量子比特量子内存下，测试权衡达到最优的Θ(n-k+1/ε)（不保真度为ε）。稳定子零度至多r的态（包括受限数量T门Clifford电路制备的态）可用O(n2^r)次单拷贝测量学习。

Conclusion: 自适应性完全弥合了稳定子态学习中单拷贝与多拷贝测量之间的样本复杂度差距，并且该机制可推广至超越精确稳定子态的近似类，如低稳定子零度态，为高效量子态学习提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Stabilizer states are central to quantum computing, underlying quantum error correction, benchmarking, and efficient classical simulation. Yet their learnability exhibits a striking gap: an $n$-qubit stabilizer state can be learned from $Θ(n)$ copies using two-copy Bell measurements, whereas non-adaptive single-copy measurements require $Ω(n^2)$ copies. Here we show that adaptivity completely closes this gap. We give a polynomial-time adaptive algorithm that learns an arbitrary $n$-qubit stabilizer state from $Θ(n)$ single-copy Clifford measurements, matching the optimal sample complexity of Bell sampling without any multi-copy measurements. The same ideas yield a sample-optimal single-copy tolerant tester and, with $k$ qubits of quantum memory, the optimal testing tradeoff $Θ(n-k+1/\varepsilon)$ at infidelity $\varepsilon$. Finally, we show that this adaptive mechanism extends beyond exact stabilizer states: states of stabilizer nullity at most $r$, including states prepared by Clifford circuits with a bounded number of $T$ gates, can be learned using $O(n2^{r})$ single-copy measurements.

</details>


### [112] [Nonlocality without entanglement for multipartite quantum measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02032)
*Satyaki Manna,Debashis Saha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文揭示了量子测量层面的"无纠缠非局域性"，证明两比特乘积测量可全局完美区分却无法在LOCC下完美区分，并刻画了自适应/非自适应、定向性等区分层级，给出多体构造与光学实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典通信与局域操作（LOCC）约束下的量子测量区分能力是量子信息基础问题。已有"无纠缠非局域性"主要针对量子态，对测量情形（process nonlocality without entanglement）尚缺乏系统刻画，尤其缺乏简单实例、策略层级与物理实现。

Method: 构造性方法：设计乘积测量的集合与区分任务，比较全局联合操作与LOCC下的完美区分能力；比较自适应与非自适应LOCC协议；分析协议启动方导致的定向性；构造 n 比特乘积测量对（在任意二分下即使两侧允许任意联合量子操作也无法区分）；并用基本光学元件实现量子数据隐藏。

Result: 存在一对两比特乘积测量，联合访问可完美区分，而把局域分量分发给远端并仅限LOCC时无法完美区分；给出六个 bipartite qutrit 乘积测量证明自适应LOCC严格优于受限非自适应LOCC；发现二分测量LOCC可区分性具有内在定向性；构造出全局完美可区分但在任意二分下均不可完美区分的 n 比特乘积测量对；实现基于量子过程无纠缠非局域性的量子数据隐藏。

Conclusion: 测量层面存在类似于态层面的无纠缠非局域性，且在更简单的情形中即出现；LOCC测量区分存在自适应优势与定向性层级；可推广至多体并在基础光学平台上实现量子数据隐藏。

Abstract: We show that a pair of two-qubit product measurements can be perfectly distinguished when accessed jointly, yet cannot be perfectly distinguished when their local components are distributed between distant parties restricted to local quantum operations and classical communication (LOCC). This establishes a measurement analogue of nonlocality without entanglement that, unlike its state-based counterpart, emerges in a remarkably simple setting. We further uncover a hierarchy of LOCC discrimination strategies: adaptive protocols, in which the probe used at one site may depend on classical information obtained from another, can strictly outperform restricted non-adaptive protocols, as demonstrated by a set of six bipartite qutrit product measurements. We also show that LOCC distinguishability of bipartite measurements can be intrinsically directional, depending on which party initiates the protocol. More generally, we construct pairs of $n$-qubit product measurements that are globally perfectly distinguishable but cannot be perfectly distinguished across any bipartition, even when all parties on either side of the bipartition are allowed arbitrary joint quantum operations. Finally, we show how quantum data hiding based on quantum-process nonlocality without entanglement can be realized using elementary optical components.

</details>


### [113] [Complexity and Applications of Nearest Stabilizer Product State Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02037)
*Daniel Grier,Hakop Pashayan,Luke Schaeffer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究稳定子乘积态上的最近态优化问题，给出完整复杂度分类：在Clifford群对称性下共9类集合S，除最简的2类外均为NP完全，并展示其在经典模拟、纠缠度量与低秩矩阵补全中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题（给定n量子比特稳定子态|ψ⟩与单量子比特稳定子态集合S，最大化|⟨ψ|φ1,...,φn⟩|²）在不同S下具有多种应用价值：可收紧某些经典模拟算法的运行时间上界、构造纠缠度量、以及刻画低秩矩阵补全的复杂度。作者希望统一理解并界定该问题的计算复杂度。

Method: 将最近稳定子乘积态问题形式化为上述最大化问题；利用Clifford群对称性对集合S进行等价类归约，得到9种本质不同的S；随后对各情形进行复杂度分析与归约证明。

Result: 在Clifford群对称性意义下共存在9种不同的集合S；其中除最简单的两类外，其余全部为NP完全。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了最近稳定子乘积态问题的完整复杂度分类，并表明通过选取不同S可服务于经典模拟运行时间界、纠缠度量及低秩矩阵补全等多个方向。

Abstract: Consider the following optimization problem over stabilizer product states: given an $n$-qubit stabilizer state $\left|ψ\right\rangle$ and a set of single-qubit stabilizer states $S$, maximize $\left|\langle ψ| φ_1, \ldots, φ_n \rangle\right|^2$ over single-qubit stabilizer states $\left|φ_i\right\rangle \in S$. By varying the set $S$, we show that solutions to this problem can be useful in a variety of settings: tighter runtime bounds for certain classical simulation algorithms; measures of entanglement; and the complexity of low-rank matrix completion. Moreover, we give a complete complexity classification of this nearest stabilizer product state problem. After accounting for the symmetries in the Clifford group, there are $9$ distinct possible sets $S$, and we show that all but the two simplest of these are $\NP$-complete.

</details>


### [114] [Fermionic Gaussianity can be tested with mode-independent sample-complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02050)
*Maxwell West,Martin Larocca*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用的上界技术，用于界定"接受态"的接受概率，从而给出判定问题样本复杂度的上界；并以费米高斯态与Slater行列式态为例，证明Bell采样以最优Θ(ε^{-2})复杂度判定费米高斯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判定未知量子态是否属于或远离某一给定态族是量子信息论的自然问题。此类问题通常存在自然的2副本测试，对属于目标族的态总是接受，但对远离目标族的态，其上界（接受概率）往往难以分析，因此难以确定判定问题的样本复杂度。

Method: 提出一种简单的新技术，用于对一般态族的上界接受概率进行估计，进而给出判定问题本身样本复杂度的上界。将该框架应用于费米高斯态族，分析著名的Bell采样测试过程；并进一步推广到Slater行列式态族的判定问题。

Result: 证明判定未知纯n模态是费米高斯的、还是与所有高斯态在迹距离上至少ε远，可用最优副本数Θ(ε^{-2})完成；并证明Bell采样测试在此意义下是最优的，即使与允许任意集体测量和自适应测量的协议相比亦然。对于Slater行列式态族，类似判定问题同样可用与模数无关的样本复杂度求解。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个通用的接受概率上界框架，能有效刻画态族判定问题的样本复杂度，并确立了Bell采样对费米高斯性检验的最优性，同时将结果拓展至Slater行列式态族。

Abstract: Deciding whether an unknown quantum state either belongs to, or is far from, a given family of states is a natural question of quantum information theory. In many such cases, there is a natural 2-copy test which always accepts whenever the state indeed belongs to the target family; it is often much more difficult, however, to bound the probability of acceptance for states that are far from the family. Here we develop a simple new technique for upper bounding this probability for general families, and therefore upper bounding the sample-complexity of the decision problem itself. As a particularly striking example of this framework, we show that deciding whether an unknown pure $n$-mode state is fermionic Gaussian, or at least $\varepsilon$-far in trace distance from all Gaussian states, can be accomplished using the optimal number $Θ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ of copies. Remarkably, our analysis applies to the well-known Bell sampling procedure for testing fermionic Gaussianity, which we therefore show to be optimal, even compared to protocols making arbitrary collective and adaptive measurements. As a second example, we show that the analogous decision problem for the family of Slater determinant states can also be solved with mode-independent sample-complexity.

</details>


### [115] [Sequential Capacity of Quantum Processes with Finite Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02068)
*Yibin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"顺序响应容量"量化有限内存量子设备在长时间运行下的响应复杂度，证明容量随运行步数 $K$ 呈 $K\log K$ 增长，并刻画噪声如何削弱这一对数增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子设备在内部内存固定、运行时间延长时其响应复杂度如何增长，并寻找刻画该复杂度的定量指标与容量上限。

Method: 定义顺序响应容量：在给定响应概率间隔下，用多次全新运行的自适应测试阶段可持续区分不同过程的最大阶段数。对固定系统与内存规模，建立容量与运行长度、概率分辨率之间的紧致关系；构造单可见量子比特上时间依赖相位旋转（无额外内部内存、响应概率恰为 0 或 1）的方案，并与固定基测量的经典随机过程对比；进一步分析由经典标签选定的相位序列在已知独立泡利噪声下的容量变化。

Result: 固定分辨率下容量按 $K\log K$ 增长；构造方案达到该增长，且每个测试响应概率恰为 0 或 1；同测试条件下，固定基测量的经典随机过程在固定规模与分辨率下仅具线性容量；对经典标签选择的相位序列，证明在理想控制与校正后弱残余相位噪声下，固定小概率间隔时容量上下界匹配，并识别出残余相位翻转概率的倒数即限制额外对数增长的相干时间尺度。

Conclusion: 量子顺序响应容量可超越经典线性容量，实现 $K\log K$ 的对数增强；该增强受残余相位噪声限制，其相干时间尺度由残余相位翻转概率倒数刻画。

Abstract: How complex can the responses of a quantum device become as it runs longer with a fixed internal memory? We quantify this complexity through sequential response capacity: how many adaptive testing stages, each using a fresh run, can continue to separate possible processes by a prescribed gap in response probabilities. For fixed system and memory sizes, we establish a tight law relating this capacity to run length and probability resolution. At fixed resolution, the capacity grows on the order of $K\log K$, where $K$ is the number of time steps in each run. Our construction attains this growth using time-dependent phase rotations on a single visible qubit with no additional internal memory; its tests give response probabilities exactly zero or one. Under the same tests, classical stochastic processes that measure in a fixed basis at every step have only linear capacity at fixed sizes and resolution. For phase sequences selected by a stored classical label, we then quantify how known independent Pauli noise changes this logarithmic enhancement. With ideal controls and weak residual phase noise after correction, we prove matching capacity bounds at a fixed small probability gap. These bounds identify the inverse residual phase-flip probability as the coherence timescale that limits the extra logarithmic growth.

</details>


### [116] [Universal Bound and Phase Transition in Many-Body Fermionic Non-Gaussianity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02075)
*Masahiro Hoshino,Ryota Matsuda,Yuto Ashida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文以magic Rényi熵(MRE)度量费米子非高斯性，给出其通用上界与Haar均值，证明大系统极限下典型Haar随机态达到每Majorana ln(4/3)的最大MRE密度，并揭示SYK演化中存在一级相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子非高斯性是通用量子计算的资源，可由量子多体相互作用产生；但缺乏对其度量、上限及增长动力学的系统理解，需要以合适度量刻画典型态的极限与演化行为。

Method: 以magic Rényi熵(MRE)作为非高斯性度量；推导MRE的通用上界与Haar随机均值，分析相同协方差矩阵态间MRE差异；在SYK哈密顿量下对高斯态做虚时与实时演化，扫描时长并考察MRE评估所涉及四副本间的置换对称性。

Result: 证明典型Haar随机态在大系统极限达到每Majorana ln(4/3)的最大MRE密度；发现协方差矩阵相同的态其MRE可相差广延量级；SYK演化中随虚时与实时时长变化出现一级相变，表现为MRE密度拐点及四副本置换对称性自发破缺。

Conclusion: 费米子非高斯性的增长可由SYK动力学调控并在一级相变处发生定性变化，该相变不体现在热自由能中，说明非高斯性携带超出热力学量的独立信息。

Abstract: Fermionic non-Gaussianity is a resource for universal quantum computation that can be generated by interactions in quantum many-body systems. Using the magic Rényi entropy (MRE) as a measure of non-Gaussianity, we derive its universal upper bound and Haar mean, proving that typical Haar-random states attain the maximal MRE density of $\ln(4/3)$ per Majorana in the large-system limit. We also show that the MRE can differ extensively between states with identical covariance matrices. To investigate how non-Gaussianity grows toward this maximal density, we evolve a Gaussian state in imaginary and real time under the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev Hamiltonian. By varying the imaginary- and real-time durations, we identify a first-order transition marked by a kink in the MRE density and spontaneous breaking of the permutation symmetry among the four copies used to evaluate the MRE. This transition represents a qualitative change in the non-Gaussianity of the state that is not reflected in the thermal free energy.

</details>


### [117] [A computational phase diagram for the transverse field Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02079)
*Thuy-Duong Vuong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究横场Ising模型配分函数与Gibbs态的可计算性：当谱宽与横场强度满足 $\Delta(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(\beta\eta)}{\eta}\le1$ 时给出多项式时间随机经典算法；越过该阈值则 $\exp(o(n))$ 近似为NP难，并给出Gibbs态Pauli观测量近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横场Ising模型配分函数的经典/量子可计算性存在明确的复杂性相变，理解何时可高效近似、何时变为难解，对量子多体物理与哈密顿量复杂性理论具有重要意义。本文旨在刻画这一可计算性阈值并给出相应算法。

Method: 定义哈密顿量 $H=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i,j\in[n]}J_{ij}Z_iZ_j+\sum_i h_i^z Z_i+\eta\sum_i X_i$，以 $\Delta(J)=\lambda_{\max}(J)-\lambda_{\min}(J)$ 为相互作用矩阵J的谱宽。在条件 $\Delta(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(\beta\eta)}{\eta}\le1$ 下，设计随机经典算法逼近配分函数 $Z(\beta)=\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-\beta H})$ 与Gibbs态 $\rho_\beta$ 的Pauli串观测量；并通过复杂性归约证明阈值之外的难解性。

Result: 当 $\Delta(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(\beta\eta)}{\eta}\le1$ 时，算法以相对误差 $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$ 逼近 $Z(\beta)$，时间为 $n$、$\beta$、模型参数与 $\varepsilon^{-1}$ 的多项式；同一区间内可对Gibbs态的Pauli串观测量实现任意小加性误差近似，若观测量在X基下对角（$P\in\{I,X\}^{\otimes n}$）则可达到任意小相对误差。当 $\Delta(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(\beta\eta)}{\eta}>1$ 时，逼近 $Z(\beta)$ 至 $\exp(o(n))$ 乘性因子为NP难，故在标准复杂性假设下不太可能有多项式经典或量子算法。

Conclusion: 配分函数的可高效近似性由量 $\Delta(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(\beta\eta)}{\eta}$ 决定：不超过1时经典可高效计算，超过1时变难解，从而刻画了横场Ising模型的可计算性相变，并将结果推广到Gibbs态观测量的近似。

Abstract: We study the transverse field Ising model, defined by the Hamiltonian $H =\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i, j\in [n]} J_{ij} Z_i Z_j +\sum_{i=1}^n h_i^z Z_i + η\sum_{i} X_i$ where $J $ is the symmetric interaction matrix, and $η$ is the transverse field strength. Let $Δ(J)=λ_{\max}(J)-λ_{\min}(J)$ be the spectral width of $J.$ When the inverse temperature $β\geq0$ satisfies $Δ(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(βη)}η\leq1$, we give a randomized classical algorithm that approximates the partition function $Z(β)=\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-βH})$ to a given relative error $ε\in(0,1)$ in time polynomial in $n$, $β$, the model parameters, and $ε^{-1}$. When $ Δ(J) \cdot \frac{\tanh(βη)}η > 1 ,$ we show that approximating $ Z(β)$ within an $\exp(o(n))$-multiplicative factor is $\textbf{NP}$-hard, and thus unlikely to admit an efficient classical or quantum algorithms under standard complexity theoretic assumptions. Furthermore, in the regime $Δ(J)\cdot \frac{\tanh(βη)}η\leq 1,$ we provide an efficient randomized classical algorithm that approximates Pauli string observables of the Gibbs state $ ρ_β= \frac{e^{-βH}}{\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-βH})}$ within an arbitrarily small additive error. In the special case when the observable is also diagonal in the $X$-basis, i.e. $P \in \{I, X\}^{\otimes n}$, the algorithm further achieves arbitrarily small relative error.

</details>


### [118] [Strong Simulation of 1D Quantum Circuits via Reduced Transition Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02082)
*Matilde Grassi,Stefano Carignano,Luca Tagliacozzo,Jacopo De Nardis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出Sweeping RTM算法，用于一维混沌砖墙线路特定输出概率的强模拟，数值显示所需键维随时间的增长在可及窗口内呈亚指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络模拟量子多体系统时，空间纠缠增长严重限制混沌幺正线路下纯态的直接演化。本文转而针对更具体的目标：以有限相对精度近似强模拟一维混沌砖墙线路的指定输出概率。

Method: 给定输入、输出比特串 y 与 x，计算 p(x|y)=|<x|U(T)|y>|^2。采用Sweeping RTM算法，一种基于约化转移矩阵（RTM）的横向张量网络收缩方法；算法联合压缩左右时间边界态，寻求在空间切割间以及随键维增大而收敛的输出概率。

Result: 对于固定相对目标精度下的一维混沌砖墙线路，数值证据表明获得稳定估计所需键维在可及时间窗口内呈亚指数增长。

Conclusion: 研究为混沌量子线路的经典概率查询提供了直接途径，有望应用于基准测试与学习任务。

Abstract: Tensor networks are powerful tools for simulating quantum many-body systems, but the growth of spatial entanglement severely limits the direct evolution of pure states under chaotic unitary circuits. Here we consider a more targeted task: the approximate strong simulation of a specified output probability of a 1D chaotic brick-wall circuit at finite relative precision. Given input and output bit strings $\by$ and $\bx$, we evaluate $p(\bx|\by)=|\bra{\bx}U(T)\ket{\by}|^2$ using the Sweeping RTM algorithm, a transverse tensor-network contraction based on reduced transition matrices (RTMs). The algorithm compresses the left and right temporal boundary states jointly, seeking an output probability that converges across spatial cuts and as the bond dimension is increased. For chaotic one-dimensional brick-wall circuits at a fixed relative target precision, we find numerical evidence that the bond dimension required to obtain stable estimates grows subexponentially over the accessible time window. Our findings open a direct route to classical probability queries for chaotic quantum circuits, with potential applications to benchmarking and learning tasks.

</details>


### [119] [Stationarity as a One-Mode Constraint on Quantum Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02090)
*Itai Panas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出正则化Hartree-Fock (reg-HF) 框架，论证定态时间演化在能量标量性质上的"单一性"，并用氦原子作为最小非平凡检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定态即使内部响应跨越巨大希尔伯特空间，其动力学仍只有一个相位因子和一个标量能量。作者关注的不是绝对相位的数值或可观测性，而是定态时间演化的"单一性"这一性质能否在微观复杂谱下仍成立。

Method: 在reg-HF中，先建立与定态平均场的联系，再做单模约化：(1) 由高斯HF表示诱导的原积相对坐标高斯测度固定协方差，该协方差同时是Fisher信息极小、Shannon微分熵极大分布，且是Stam不等式的等号情形；(2) Coulomb算符允许相容的高斯尺度分解；(3) virial完备化将正则化对位移转为定态相容响应；(4) 提出单模排他公理——保留配对模正交分量所携带的协方差被排除在终端标量能量性质之外，但不声称无限制响应是一维的。

Result: 氦原子检验中，无参数保留Coulomb载子 -δ/e 与有限Z插入 8δ/9 能紧密复现独立的类氦系数与能量；显式Bethe-Goldstone计算显示出宽的非单分母返回谱，但其标量能量恰可做矩压缩。

Conclusion: 结果将微观谱复杂度与单模能量充分性区分开来：定态能量标量性质可与宽广的返回谱共存，微观测度的复杂性并不妨碍能量层面的单模描述。

Abstract: A stationary quantum state has one dynamical phase factor and one scalar energy even when its internal response spans a large Hilbert space. Our argument concerns not the value or observability of an absolute phase, but the one-ness of stationary time evolution. In regularized Hartree--Fock (reg-HF), the connection to the stationary mean field is established before any one-mode reduction: the primitive-product relative-coordinate Gaussian measure induced by the Gaussian HF representation fixes a covariance and, at that covariance, is simultaneously the minimum-Fisher-information and maximum-Shannon-differential-entropy distribution and the equality case of the Stam bound. The Coulomb operator admits a compatible Gaussian scale resolution, and virial completion converts the resulting regularized pair displacement into a stationary-compatible response. Stationarity motivates a one-mode constraint on the terminal scalar correlation response. The decisive reg-HF hypothesis is a one-mode exclusion axiom: covariance carried by response components orthogonal to the retained pair mode is excluded from the terminal scalar energy property, without asserting that the unrestricted response is one dimensional. Helium provides the minimal nontrivial test. The parameter-free retained Coulomb carrier $-δ/e$ and finite-$Z$ insertion $8δ/9$ closely reproduce independent He-like coefficients and energies, while an explicit Bethe--Goldstone calculation displays a broad non-single-denominator return spectrum even though its scalar energy is exactly moment-compressible. The result separates microscopic spectral complexity from one-mode energetic sufficiency.

</details>


### [120] [One-Way Quantum Symmetric Private Information Retrieval Protocol From A Single Database Server Using NISQ Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02093)
*Xiang Zou,H. F. Chau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种在单服务器、信息论安全下的对称私有信息检索（SPIR）方案，利用仅具备NISQ设备且无长期量子存储的双方，实现了对全能量窃听者的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单服务器SPIR在无限制量子能力下不可能实现，因此需要引入物理限制假设（如有限或噪声量子存储）来突破不可能性结果，并实现更严格的安全性。

Method: 服务器单向发送BB84态；用户无歧义判别后获知随机子集比特，经联合随机置换分块，公布私选块中的确定性位置；服务器对每块这些位置进行哈希并取随机奇偶校验；仅需在BB84设备上增加一个带辅助模的被动线性光学测量。

Result: 证明了窃听者保密性、通过公布集合大小规则的数据库隐私以及针对诚实准备BB84态的服务器用户隐私；对单光子及容忍噪声信道下小多光子项成立；10^4比特数据库、数据库隐私10^-6时约千量子比特块足够；量化了认证任意无记忆用户与承诺置换所需的更大辅助传输（有限密钥）；数据库隐私可扩展到诱骗态弱相干脉冲，仿真显示界近乎紧致。

Conclusion: 该方案在仅NISQ设备、无长期量子存储、逐到达即测的严格限制下实现了信息论安全的单服务器SPIR，且仅需对BB84设备作微小增补，具有良好的实用性。

Abstract: Symmetric private information retrieval (SPIR) lets an inquirer obtain one specific bit only, without leaking any information about its index to the database owner. With a single server, this is impossible for parties with unrestricted quantum power. We show that it becomes possible, with information-theoretic security, when the server and the user hold only noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices without long-term quantum memory and measure each qubit on arrival, while the eavesdropper remains all-powerful; this is a stricter form of the memory restriction of the bounded- and noisy-quantum-storage models. The server sends BB84 states one way; the user discriminates them unambiguously and so knows a random subset of the bits. After a joint random permutation into blocks, the user announces the conclusive locations of one privately chosen block, and the server hashes and takes a random parity of these locations in every block; the permutation makes it exponentially unlikely that one set of locations fits two blocks. We prove secrecy against the eavesdropper, database privacy through an explicit size rule for the announced set, and user privacy against a server who prepares the prescribed BB84 states and announces truthfully, exactly for single photons and up to a small multi-photon term over a tolerated noise channel. The only addition to BB84 equipment is a passive linear-optical measurement with one ancillary mode. For a $10^{4}$-bit database and database privacy $10^{-6}$, blocks of about a thousand qubits suffice over a tolerated noise channel for a user who applies the prescribed measurement; certifying an arbitrary memoryless user and committing to the permutation need larger auxiliary transmissions, which we quantify with finite keys. Database privacy extends to decoy-state weak coherent pulses, and simulations show that the bound is nearly tight.

</details>


### [121] [Learning Many-Body Hamiltonians Using a Local Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02157)
*Suying Liu,Zitai Xu,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Xiaodi Wu,Yu-Xin Wang,Zhi-Yuan Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种仅需单一可测量子比特即可学习N量子比特有界度两体哈密顿量全部O(N)个独立参数的方案，达到海森堡极限精度，并通过量子信号处理合成的鲁棒SWAP门实现相干态传输，在链上实现总查询时间Õ(N)且匹配信息传播下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿量学习协议通常假设能够直接测量整个系统，这在实际中难以满足。作者希望回答：能否在只有局部测量接口（单个可测量量子比特）的限制下，仍高效地学习大量多体哈密顿量参数。

Method: 利用快速单量子比特控制和连通的参考骨架，通过量子信号处理合成的鲁棒SWAP门，将远处哈密顿量参数作用下演化的量子态相干传输到可测量子比特上；该传输无需对中间链路的哈密顿量参数进行预先校准。进一步采用并行学习架构进行参数估计。

Result: 单个可测量量子比特即可学习有界度两体哈密顿量的全部O(N)个独立参数，精度达到海森堡极限；在N量子比特链上总查询时间为Õ(N)，且保持海森堡极限精度标度。这些标度在链上（除对数因子外）与基本精度极限和信息传播下界相匹配。该框架可推广到任意有界度相互作用图。

Conclusion: 研究证明可以通过单一局部测量接口学习大量多体哈密顿量参数，为可扩展的哈密顿量学习提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Hamiltonian learning provides a systematic framework for reconstructing unknown quantum dynamics. However, existing protocols typically assume direct measurement access to the entire system. With fast single-qubit control and a connected reference backbone, we show that a single measurable qubit suffices to learn all $O(N)$ independent parameters of a bounded-degree two-body Hamiltonian on $N$ qubits at the Heisenberg limit. Crucially, our protocol uses robust SWAP gates synthesized by quantum signal processing, enabling coherent transfer of states evolving under distant Hamiltonian parameters to the measurable qubit. This transfer requires no prior calibration of the Hamiltonian parameters of the intermediate links. A parallel learning architecture achieves total query time $\widetilde{O}(N)$ for an $N$-qubit chain, while retaining Heisenberg-limited precision scaling. On the chain, these scalings match the fundamental precision and information-propagation lower bounds up to logarithmic factors. The framework further extends to arbitrary bounded-degree interaction graphs. Our results establish a scalable route to learning an extensive number of many-body Hamiltonian parameters through only a local measurement interface.

</details>


### [122] [Beyond Light Cones: State Preparation Complexity in Quantum Spin Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02166)
*Omar Al-Ghattas,David Gamarnik,Bobak T Kiani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于有效剖面复杂度的量子态制备复杂度方法，突破光锥界，给出稠密p-spin哈密顿量基态能量的下界与门数/深度-宽度/纠缠等资源下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于电路光锥的界无法刻画可丢弃辅助比特、Clifford电路等非局域操作对态制备复杂度的限制，需要新的复杂度度量来研究稠密量子p-spin哈密顿量基态能量的可达性。

Method: 引入由Pauli剖面度量熵导出的类有效剖面复杂度；结合Berta等非对称量子de Finetti定理与高斯过程熵界进行证明。

Result: 有效剖面复杂度一致有二次界的类其基态能量与类态能量存在正比于√n的分离；亚二次时不能超越乘积态基准。达到近基态能量需Ω(n²/logn)个单双比特门（允许可丢弃辅助比特），并得到深度-宽度权衡、纠缠深度与矩阵乘积态键维下界、Parham魔术层级各层（总宽度O(n)）的双向障碍；一阶反向魔术允许任意多清洁辅助比特；Clifford+T含o(n)个T门相对乘积稳定子态无领先阶能量优势。

Conclusion: 有效剖面复杂度提供了超越光锥的统一框架，可导出稠密p-spin基态制备的多种资源下界，并覆盖Clifford与魔术层级等非局域电路结构。

Abstract: We introduce a method for studying state preparation complexity in dense quantum $p$-spin Hamiltonians on $n$ qubits, going beyond bounds based only on circuit lightcones. The key input is the class's effective profile complexity, which is derived from the metric entropy of its Pauli profiles. These profiles record expectations of all Pauli operators supported on exactly $p$ qubits. Classes with uniformly bounded quadratic effective profile complexity remain separated from the ground-state energy by a positive multiple of $\sqrt n$ for sufficiently large fixed $p$. At subquadratic effective profile complexity, the class cannot outperform a suitable benchmark class at leading order, with product states providing a universal benchmark. The proof combines an adaptation of a nonsymmetric quantum de Finetti theorem of Berta et al. (arXiv:1810.12197) with Gaussian process entropy bounds.
  Applying this framework, we show that attaining near-ground-state energy requires $Ω(n^2/\log n)$ one- and two-qubit gates, even with arbitrary discardable ancillas. We also obtain depth-width tradeoffs, entanglement-depth and matrix product state bond-dimension lower bounds, and obstructions for both orientations at every fixed level of Parham's magic hierarchy (arXiv:2504.19966), with total circuit width $O(n)$. In first-level reverse magic, a shallow circuit is followed by an unrestricted Clifford circuit. The latter can spread local observables across the system, preventing a direct application of small-lightcone bounds. For this first-level class, our bounds also allow arbitrarily many clean ancillas at fixed shallow-circuit depth. A sharper benchmark shows that Clifford+$T$ circuits with $o(n)$ $T$-gates have no leading-order energy advantage over product stabilizer states, even with unrestricted Clifford operations and arbitrary discardable ancillas.

</details>


### [123] [Polynomial-time classical and quantum simulation of quantum impurity models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02167)
*Jiaqing Jiang,Nathan Ju,Ojas Parekh,Chaithanya Rayudu,Andrew Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究量子杂质模型的计算复杂性，划定了经典与量子可处理性的边界：静态性质可经典高效计算，而动力学性质经典难解但量子易解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子杂质模型既是相互作用量子物质的重要范例，也是现代电子结构方法的关键计算基元，描述与大型非相互作用浴耦合的小型相互作用费米子子系统。作者旨在明确该类问题经典与量子可处理性的分界线。

Method: 通过复杂性理论分析，给出经典算法并刻画问题的复杂性类。针对静态性质提出多项式时间算法：（1）以加性精度δ估计基态能量；（2）以相对精度δ估计逆温度β下的配分函数。针对动力学性质，以非平衡格林函数为例进行复杂性归约分析。

Result: 静态性质方面：基态能量估计在poly(n,δ^{-1})时间内完成，将此前已知的拟多项式时间改进为多项式时间；配分函数估计在poly(n,β,δ^{-1})时间内达到相对精度δ，首次为热平衡下模拟杂质模型建立严格的多项式时间保证。动力学方面：计算非平衡格林函数即使在有限温度下也蕴含量子计算的完整能力，表明其对经典计算机难解、对量子计算机易解。

Conclusion: 综合结果表明，在计算静态性质上不存在超多项式量子加速的可能性，但在模拟非平衡杂质物理方面存在实现量子优势的途径。

Abstract: Quantum impurity models are paradigmatic models of interacting quantum matter, as well as key computational primitives for modern electronic-structure methods. They describe a small subsystem of interacting fermions coupled to a large, noninteracting bath. We perform a comprehensive study of the computational complexity of simulating impurity models, delineating the boundary between classical and quantum tractability for this class of problems. Our main finding is that static properties of quantum impurity models can be calculated efficiently on a classical computer. Specifically, we give classical algorithms that (1) estimate the ground-state energy to additive precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,δ^{-1})$, and (2) estimate the partition function at inverse temperature $β$ to relative precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,β,δ^{-1})$, where $n$ is the system size. These results improve the previous best-known complexity for ground-state energy estimation from quasipolynomial to polynomial time, while establishing for the first time rigorous polynomial-time guarantees for simulating impurity models in thermal equilibrium. On the other hand, we find that simulating dynamical properties of impurity models is hard for classical computers but easy on a quantum computer. As a canonical example, we show that computing their nonequilibrium Green's functions captures the full power of quantum computation, even at finite temperature. Taken together, our results rule out superpolynomial quantum speedups for computing static properties, but provide an avenue for quantum advantage in simulating impurity physics out of equilibrium.

</details>


### [124] [Robust exponential lower bounds for fermionic and bosonic Gaussian ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02172)
*Fuchuan Wei,Kong-Wing Wu,Zhengwei Liu,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究玻色与费米系统中高斯秩问题，证明任意纯非高斯态的张量幂的近似边界高斯秩随模式数至少指数增长，从而表明非高斯性普遍导致指数级高斯分解复杂度，限制了基于分解的经典模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟的能力与局限是理解量子计算优势的核心。基于相干分解为经典可处理自由态（其分解秩决定模拟复杂度）是主流模拟范式，但对秩给出强下界极为困难，如量子比特稳定子秩问题。作者希望在高斯（玻色/费米）版本中建立稳健的指数下界。

Method: 对费米子，将问题归约到四个模式并结合马约拉纳谱界；对玻色子，采用高斯后选择方法与基于熵的秩界。证明针对有限模式上任意纯非高斯态（费米情形要求确定宇称）的张量幂的近似边界高斯秩。

Result: 证明任意纯非高斯态的张量幂的近似边界高斯秩在任意固定范数误差小于1时至少指数增长；给出四模式费米GHZ态与玻色单光子态的显式指数下界；玻色结果无需对目标态能量作假设。

Conclusion: 非高斯性普遍意味着指数级高斯分解复杂度，为玻色与费米系统基于分解的经典模拟设置了根本性限制。

Abstract: The power and limitations of classical simulation are central to understanding quantum computational advantages. A leading simulation paradigm is based on coherent decomposition into classically tractable free states where the decomposition rank determines the simulation complexity. Proving strong lower bounds on this number is a notoriously difficult and mathematically rich problem, as exemplified by the qubit stabilizer rank problem. Here, we study the Gaussian version of this problem in both bosonic and fermionic systems and establish robust exponential lower bounds on Gaussian rank. In particular, we prove that for every pure non-Gaussian state on finitely many modes, with definite parity in the fermionic case, the approximate border Gaussian rank of its tensor powers grows at least exponentially at any fixed norm error below one. Our proofs combine reduction to four modes with Majorana spectral bounds for fermions, and Gaussian postselection with an entropy-based rank bound for bosons. As concrete examples, we derive explicit exponential lower bounds for the four-mode fermionic GHZ state and the bosonic single-photon state. The bosonic results require no assumption on the energy of the target state. Our results show that non-Gaussianity universally entails exponential Gaussian decomposition complexity, setting fundamental limitations on decomposition-based classical simulation of bosonic and fermionic systems.

</details>


### [125] [From Permutation Symmetry to Communication Bounds and Additivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02176)
*Zahra Baghali Khanian,Debbie Leung,Graeme Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：对任意有限维无记忆信道，全置换不变性使每use相干信息的优化收敛到单次最大值并在有限块长处达到上确界；同时给出纯源纠缠生成的强逆定理，并揭示完全有界范数与sandwiched Rényi信道量共享的变分结构，将对称性方法扩展到输出熵控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨信道使用的关联可提升量子通信速率，使得在任意大块上进行优化成为确定量子容量的核心困难。作者希望刻画置换不变性的限制作用，从而简化渐近优化，并统一若干已知的可乘性/可加性结果。

Method: 利用全置换不变性、De Finetti降维（de Finetti reduction）与置换协变性，将优化限制在置换不变输入上；在共享变分框架下分析每use相干信息、纯源纠缠生成保真度、完全有界范数及sandwiched Rényi信道量，并推广到Rényi阶大于1与von Neumann熵的输出熵界。

Result: 优化后的每use相干信息收敛到单次最大值，并在有限块长处取得上确界；有限块超可加性仍可能，而渐近优化归结为单次信道使用。对置换不变输入的纯源纠缠生成，任意高于单次相干信息最大值的固定速率下保真度趋于零（强逆）。完全有界范数与sandwiched Rényi信道量在共享变分框架内获得可乘性与可加性的系统替代证明；对置换不变输入，输出熵每use无法渐近低于单次最小值。

Conclusion: 置换不变性显著限制了多use关联带来的优势，使渐近通信优化退化为单次使用，并将对称性方法从通信界扩展到输出熵控制，为量子容量、纠缠生成及信道判别等提供统一框架。

Abstract: Correlations across channel uses can improve quantum communication rates, making optimization over arbitrarily large blocks a central difficulty in determining quantum capacity. We show how full permutation invariance limits this advantage for every finite-dimensional memoryless channel. The optimized coherent information per use converges to the single-use maximum and consequently attains its supremum at a finite block length. Thus finite-block superadditivity remains possible, while the asymptotic optimization reduces to a single channel use. We also establish a strong converse for pure-source entanglement generation with permutation-invariant inputs: at any fixed rate above the single-use coherent-information maximum, the fidelity tends to zero. Beyond these communication bounds, we identify a common structure underlying completely bounded norms and the sandwiched Rényi channel quantities governing discrimination against a replacer and entanglement-assisted communication. De Finetti reduction and permutation covariance yield systematic alternative proofs of their known multiplicativity and additivity results within a shared variational framework. We further show that, for permutation-invariant inputs, correlations cannot sustain an asymptotic reduction in output entropy per use below the single-use minimum. This entropy limit holds for Rényi orders greater than one and for the von Neumann entropy, extending the role of symmetry from communication bounds to the control of output entropy.

</details>


### [126] [Learning SYK Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02178)
*Anurag Anshu,Srinivasan Arunachalam,Sitan Chen,Yeongwoo Hwang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究从Gibbs态样本中学习稠密SYK哈密顿量的问题，突破了几何局域性与有界相互作用度等传统假设，利用模型随机平均场结构实现了多项式样本学习与拟多项式时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿量学习算法依赖几何局域性或相互作用度有界，而SYK模型中每个四体相互作用与Θ(n³)个其他项重叠，导致这些算法不适用，因此需要新的方法克服这一障碍。

Method: 利用SYK模型的随机平均场结构，在任意常数温度下证明以高概率（对SYK耦合）可用多项式数量的样本将整个哈密顿量学习到逆多项式精度；并在逆温度为足够小常数时构造拟多项式时间学习算法。

Result: 在任意常数温度下，以高概率（对SYK耦合）可用多项式样本数学习整个哈密顿量至逆多项式精度；当逆温度为足够小常数时，得到定性不同于样本高效算法的拟多项式时间学习算法。

Conclusion: SYK哈密顿量的稠密性障碍可通过随机平均场结构克服，实现了多项式样本复杂度与拟多项式时间复杂度的学习算法。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning the dense Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) Hamiltonian from copies of its Gibbs state. Existing algorithms for Hamiltonian learning typically rely on geometric locality or bounded interaction degree and therefore do not apply to SYK, where each quartic interaction overlaps with $Θ(n^3)$ others. We show that this obstruction can be overcome by exploiting the random mean-field structure of the model. At any constant temperature, we prove that with high probability over the SYK couplings, the entire Hamiltonian can be learned to inverse-polynomial accuracy using polynomially many samples. Furthermore, when the inverse temperature is restricted to be a sufficiently small constant, we construct a quasipolynomial-time learning algorithm which is qualitatively different from the sample-efficient algorithm.

</details>


### [127] [Single-Particle Spectral Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02183)
*Adrian Chapman,Charles Derby,Steven T. Flammia,Yeongwoo Hwang,Joel Klassen,Calum McCartney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究被未知全局酉算符遮蔽的非相互作用哈密顿量学习问题，证明其DQC1困难，并提出SPICES算法高效恢复谱分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在材料建模中，平均场理论、格林函数和密度泛函理论等方法依赖准粒子谱。然而实际系统中非相互作用结构常被未知全局酉变换遮蔽，直接恢复能量权重而不重建该酉算符具有重要意义。本文旨在探索这一新设定下的哈密顿量学习可行性。

Method: 作者将任务形式化为学习 H=U†(∑E_i n_i + H_O)U 中的权重E_i，其中I为非相互作用模式集合，H_O作用在不相交模式集合O上。首先证明该问题在计算复杂度上属于DQC1困难。随后提出SPICES（single-particle inference via contour estimators），结合Hadamard测试与新颖的经典后处理程序，利用单粒子能量与复逆温度下配分函数零点所对应的傅里叶频率之间的对应关系来恢复谱分布。

Result: 证明该非相互作用结构学习问题是DQC1困难的，表明即使存在明显简化仍具计算挑战性。SPICES在一定自然假设下能高效地在Wasserstein距离下恢复谱分布。

Conclusion: 由于准粒子谱在下游材料计算中普遍存在，且SPICES方法简单实用，作者认为它是量子材料模拟应用的一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: We study Hamiltonian learning in a novel setting, where we are promised a physical structure that is obfuscated by a global unitary. In particular, we consider the task of learning the weights $E_i$ of a Hamiltonian $H=U^{\dagger}(\sum_{i \in I} E_i n_i + H_O)U$ comprised of a set of non-interacting modes $I$ and an arbitrary ``spectator'' $H_O$ acting on a disjoint set of modes $O$. The non-interacting structure is hidden by an unknown global unitary $U$, and our goal is to recover the $E_i$ without reconstructing $U$. Such Hamiltonians are well-motivated in the setting of materials modeling, where they find application for classical methods such as mean-field theory, Green's functions, and density functional theory. We show that this problem is DQC1-hard, despite the apparent simplification given by the non-interacting structure. We then introduce SPICES (single-particle inference via contour estimators), which combines a Hadamard test with a novel classical postprocessing procedure to efficiently recover the spectral distribution in Wasserstein distance under certain natural assumptions. The method exploits the correspondence between single-particle energies of the individual quasiparticles and the Fourier frequencies governing zeros of the partition function at complex inverse temperature. As quasiparticle spectra feature ubiquitously in downstream materials calculations, and with SPICES being simple and practical to execute, we expect that SPICES is a promising route to quantum applications in materials simulation.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Synthetic SU(3) non-Abelian gauge fields in photonic synthetic frequency dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00304)
*Bengy Tsz Tsun Wong,Shu Yang,Zehai Pang,Yi Yang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文提出利用三个耦合环形谐振器并结合调制与干涉工程化的模式转换，在合成频率维度实现SU(3)规范场，为调制谐振器系统中的高阶非阿贝尔光调控提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成规范场是调控光子波动的有力手段，尤其在调制谐振腔模式构成的合成频率维度中。然而已有工作多集中于阿贝尔或低秩非阿贝尔结构，合成频率维度中SU(3)规范场的光子实现仍然缺失，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用三个耦合环形谐振器，通过调制与干涉共同设计模式转换；构建由盖尔曼矩阵生成的矩阵值跃迁的紧束缚哈密顿量，并将其演化分解为可实验实现的光学元件；结合全波启发式数值建模进行分析。

Result: 该平台支持具有色分辨纹理的SU(3)结构能带色散，以及由SU(3)规范场塑造的波包动力学；数值建模验证了预期的光谱响应和SU(3)子空间中的类颤动（Zitterbewegung）运动。

Conclusion: 该工作将SU(3)规范场引入合成频率光子学，为在调制谐振器系统中实现更高秩非阿贝尔光调控奠定了基础。

Abstract: Synthetic gauge fields provide a powerful route to engineer photonic wave dynamics, especially in synthetic frequency dimensions formed by modulated resonator modes. While most previous realizations have focused on Abelian or lower-rank non-Abelian structures, a photonic implementation of SU(3) gauge fields in the synthetic frequency dimension has remained lacking. Here we propose a route to realize SU(3) gauge structure using three coupled ring resonators with modulation- and interference-engineered mode conversion. We formulate a tight-binding Hamiltonian with matrix-valued hopping generated by the Gell-Mann matrices and show how its evolution can be decomposed into experimentally implementable optical elements. The resulting platform supports SU(3)-structured band dispersions with color-resolved texture and wave-packet dynamics shaped by SU(3) gauge fields. Full-wave-inspired numerical modeling further confirms the expected spectral response and Zitterbewegung-like motion in SU(3) subspaces. Our results introduce SU(3) gauge fields into synthetic frequency photonics and establish a route toward higher-rank non-Abelian control of light in modulated resonator systems.

</details>


### [129] [High-power photoconductive THz emitters for fast parallel electro-optic detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00744)
*Mohsen Khalili,Robin Löscher,Anna Martinez,Abhishek Singh,Stephan Winnerl,Clara J. Saraceno*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文实现了一台高光谱太赫兹相机：采用高平均功率（38.3 mW、400 kHz）脉冲宽带太赫兹源驱动并行电光探测，在约14.5 mm视场内以2 Hz帧率对145,000个像素同步记录完整太赫兹波形的幅值与相位，突破了传统TDS在速度、视场与灵敏度间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹成像因可穿透多种光学不透明材料并依据成分加以区分而极具吸引力，其中THz-TDS能记录电场全波形，从而高灵敏地恢复幅值与相位。但将这种能力转化为快速、宽场成像一直是重大挑战，主因是传统TDS源平均功率低，迫使采集速度、视场与灵敏度之间相互妥协。

Method: 采用高平均功率、脉冲宽带太赫兹源（400 kHz重复频率下输出38.3 mW），驱动宽视场下的并行电光探测，构建高光谱太赫兹相机，同步采集完整太赫兹波形的幅值与相位。

Result: 系统在约14.5 mm视场内、以2 Hz采集速率对145,000个像素同时记录完整THz波形（幅值与相位）。

Conclusion: 结果表明高平均功率THz-TDS在宽带、高速成像方面具有潜力，可推动在线材料分辨检测，以及对不可逆、秒级时间尺度过程（如生物相关体系中的水合作用）的光谱成像等应用。

Abstract: The terahertz (THz) region is highly attractive for imaging because it penetrates many optically opaque materials and can distinguish them by composition. Among the various methods that allow us to access this spectral region, THz time-domain spectroscopy (THz-TDS) is uniquely suited to retrieve broadband information, recording the full waveform of the THz electric field to recover both amplitude and phase with high sensitivity. Translating this capability into fast, wide-field imaging has however remained a major challenge, largely because the low average power of conventional TDS sources forces a trade-off between acquisition speed, field of view, and sensitivity. Here we demonstrate a hyperspectral THz camera in which a high-average-power, pulsed broadband THz source, delivering 38.3 mW at a 400 kHz repetition rate, drives parallel electro-optic detection over a wide field of view at high acquisition rates. The system simultaneously records the full THz waveform, in amplitude and phase, across 145,000 pixels over a ~14.5 mm field of view at an acquisition rate of 2 Hz. These results demonstrate the potential of high-average-power THz-TDS for broadband, high-speed imaging, opening applications such as inline material-resolved inspection and the spectroscopic imaging of irreversible, second-timescale processes such as hydration in biologically relevant systems.

</details>


### [130] [Three-Wave Mixing as a High-Throughput Diagnostic Tool for Nonlinear Waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00747)
*Yi-Yang Liu,Kevin Chen,Kyungduk Kim,Ryotatsu Yanagimoto,Edwin Ng,Timothy P. McKenna,Marc Jankowski*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于近简并三波混频的高通量诊断方法——和频层析（SFT），用于表征波导色散和制备引起的空间不均匀性。该方法产生二维传递函数，能将色散阶数与纵向相位失配变化分开表征，并在商用PPLN波导上验证，可在数秒内完成单根波导的表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波长尺度非线性波导的发展需要能同时表征波导色散和制备引起的空间不均匀性的新诊断方法。紧约束波导虽可实现紧凑高效的非线性器件，但纳米级不均匀性会限制有效相互作用长度和转换效率。传统基于二次谐波产生的诊断方法难以将色散与纵向相位失配变化分离。

Method: 提出基于近简并三波混频的和频层析（SFT）方法。与传统二次谐波产生诊断不同，SFT产生二维传递函数，从而可将色散阶数与纵向相位失配变化分开表征，且无需相位敏感或空间分辨测量。

Result: 在商用PPLN波导上进行验证，SFT能够恢复相互作用波之间的群速度失配、基频波的群速度色散，以及纵向相位失配分布的低阶多项式近似。SFT避免了相位敏感或空间分辨测量的实验复杂性，可在数秒内表征单根波导。

Conclusion: SFT是一种高通量、快速的二阶非线性波导诊断方法，能同时表征色散与空间不均匀性，具有良好的实用性和效率。

Abstract: The recent development of wavelength-scale nonlinear waveguides highlights the need for new diagnostic methods that characterize both waveguide dispersion and fabrication-induced spatial inhomogeneities. While tightly confining waveguides enable compact and highly efficient nonlinear devices, nanometer-scale waveguide nonuniformities can limit the effective interaction length and conversion efficiency. In this work, we establish a high-throughput diagnostic method for second-order nonlinear waveguides based on near-degenerate three-wave mixing, hereafter referred to as sum-frequency tomography (SFT). In contrast with conventional diagnostics based on second-harmonic generation, SFT produces two-dimensional transfer functions that allow dispersion orders to be characterized separately from longitudinal phase-mismatch variations. We validate this technique using commercially available PPLN waveguides and show that SFT recovers the group-velocity mismatch between the interacting waves, the group-velocity dispersion of the fundamental, and a low-order polynomial approximation of the longitudinal phase-mismatch profile. SFT avoids the experimental complexities of phase-sensitive or spatially resolved measurements, enabling the characterization of individual waveguides in seconds.

</details>


### [131] [Calibration-Free Photonic Memory in a Silicon Photonics Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00748)
*Ankur Singh,Akhilesh Jaiswal*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于GlobalFoundries 45SPCLO平台非谐振可变光衰减器的免校准光子存储位单元，通过电光反馈维持互补电状态，实现选择性写入与共享读出，仿真验证了写入、保持与能耗性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光子存储位单元通常依赖谐振腔结构，需要精确的腔谐振对准与校准，增加了工艺复杂度和稳定性要求。本文旨在消除谐振对准需求，实现免校准的光子存储位单元，同时避免额外的读出衰减器或独立激光源，以降低复杂度与能耗。

Method: 采用GlobalFoundries 45SPCLO平台中的非谐振可变光衰减器构建光子存储位单元；利用电光反馈维持互补电状态，无需腔谐振对准；通过字线控制的衰减器配合差分光位线实现选择性写入；光读出复用保持状态的输入与输出分束器，无需额外读出衰减器或独立激光源；通过电路仿真评估写入、保持与能耗特性。

Result: 仿真表明，该位单元可用10 ps光脉冲、2 mW峰值功率完成写入；在光与电功率持续供给条件下可保持数据，每个逻辑状态保持时间仿真达5 s；一次状态翻转的增量写入能耗为0.562 pJ，该能耗包含电路电能及20%墙插效率下的激光贡献；位单元版图面积为147.92×219.93 μm²。

Conclusion: 该免校准光子存储位单元通过非谐振衰减器与电光反馈实现了可靠的写入与保持，并共享读出路径，简化了结构并降低了能耗，展现出在集成光子存储中的应用潜力。

Abstract: We propose a calibration-free photonic memory bitcell based on nonresonant variable optical attenuators in the GlobalFoundries 45SPCLO platform. The cell maintains complementary electrical states through electro-optical feedback without cavity-resonance alignment. Wordline-controlled attenuators enable selective writing through differential optical bitlines, while optical readout shares the holding input and output splitters, avoiding additional read attenuators or a separate laser source. Circuit simulations demonstrate writing with $10~\mathrm{ps}$ optical pulses at $2~\mathrm{mW}$ peak power. The cell retains data as long as optical and electrical power are supplied, with retention simulated for $5~\mathrm{s}$ per logic state. The evaluated transition consumes an incremental write energy of $0.562~\mathrm{pJ}$, including circuit electrical energy and the laser contribution at 20\% wall-plug efficiency. The bitcell layout occupies $147.92\times219.93~μ\mathrm{m}^{2}$.

</details>


### [132] [Parallax Depth Sectioning and 3D Reconstruction in 4D-STEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01057)
*Desheng Ma,Chia-Hao Lee,Zixiao Shi,David A. Muller,Steven E. Zeltmann*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文将4D-STEM深度信息编码/提取与层析成像和光场摄影相联系，推导了3D相位衬度传递函数，并证明tcBF与acBF深度切片可通过4D FFT非迭代快速重建，在模拟与实验数据中实现了厚弱散射样品的体成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D-STEM通过不同探测器像素形成的虚拟像之间的视差编码三维信息，但其深度信息传递机制缺乏与成熟三维成像方法（层析、光场）的统一理论联系，且需要高效、非迭代的体成像重建方法。

Method: 将4D数据视为角度切片集合得到一系列sinograms；在小角近似下将倾斜校正明场(tcBF)深度切片等同于非迭代层析重建；同时把4D数据映射为光场/全光成像，借用光场算法，通过对数据集做单次4D FFT后提取二维切片获得tcBF深度截面；进一步采用深度分辨的像差校正明场(acBF)成像。

Result: tcBF深度截面可通过单次4D FFT提取二维切片获得，在大批量深度切片重建时显著加速；acBF相较tcBF去除衬度振荡、减小响应随深度的变化，改善三维重建；在模拟与实验数据中成功分辨堆叠氧化物薄膜和纳米颗粒组装体中的不同层。

Conclusion: 来自单一4D-STEM数据集非迭代体重建，为厚、弱散射样品的成像及低剂量采集重建提供了有前景的方案；该统一视角区分了深度分辨率的几何因素与相位衬度成像中的波动光学效应。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) information is encoded in four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D-STEM) through parallax between the virtual images formed at each detector pixel. In this paper we connect the encoding and retrieval of depth-dependent information in 4D-STEM to two other widely-used 3D imaging methods, tomography and light-field photography, and derive its 3D phase contrast transfer function. Viewing the 4D data as a collection of angular slices yields a collection of sinograms, providing an intuitive visual representation of the 3D information transfer. Within the small-angle approximation, tilt-corrected bright-field (tcBF) depth sectioning is equivalent to non-iterative tomographic reconstruction. Alternatively, the 4D data also can be mapped to light-field (or plenoptic) imaging, which acquires spatially resolved diffraction data in parallel using an array of lenslets. Adapting a light-field imaging algorithm, we show that tcBF depth sections can be obtained by extracting two-dimensional slices from a single four-dimensional fast Fourier transform (4D FFT) of the dataset, yielding a substantial speedup when reconstructing a large number of depth slices. These differing perspectives distinguish the geometric aspects of depth resolution from wave-optical effects that are present in phase-contrast imaging. Depth-resolved aberration-corrected bright-field (acBF) imaging yields improved 3D reconstructions compared to tcBF, removing contrast oscillations and reducing the variation in response with depth. We demonstrate acBF depth sectioning using simulated and experimental data and resolve distinct layers in stacked oxide films and nanoparticle assemblies. These non-iterative volumetric reconstructions from a single 4D-STEM dataset show promise for improved imaging of thick, weakly-scattering samples and for reconstructing low-dose acquisitions.

</details>


### [133] [Cross-platform frequency-domain physical neural networks with identical models and parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01059)
*Zichen Xi,Jun Ji,Liyang Jin,Hsuan-Hao Lu,Wenjie Xiong,Linbo Shao*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出基于共同二阶非线性过程的频域物理神经网络（PNN），用同一套预训练参数在光学、电子（微波）和声学平台上实现跨平台部署，无需微调，四分类任务推理精度分别达97.6%、98.4%和98.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有频域PNN虽具备器件少、鲁棒性强、精度高等优势，但多局限于特定器件或平台，依赖硬件相关模型和制造后参数调优，缺乏跨平台通用性。

Method: 利用各平台共享的二阶非线性过程构建统一频域PNN模型，在光学、微波、声波三类平台直接部署同一预训练参数，不进行参数微调，并在统一四分类任务上开展验证和跨域性能指标对比。

Result: 统一四分类任务中，光学平台推理精度97.6%，电子平台98.4%，力学（声学）平台98.2%，均保持高精度，并系统比较了三类物理域的相关系能指标。

Conclusion: 频域PNN可借助共享二阶非线性实现跨物理平台通用部署，为构建跨平台的通用神经网络提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Frequency-domain physical neural networks (PNNs) have emerged as a promising analog computing paradigm, achieving a reduced number of devices, enhanced robustness, and high inference accuracy. However, existing demonstrations across various physical domains, such as optics, acoustics, and electronics, are mostly specific to the target devices or platforms, usually requiring hardware-dependent models and post-fabrication parameter tuning. Here, we demonstrate cross-platform implementations of frequency-domain PNN across different wave-based computing platforms using identical, pre-trained parameters. By exploiting shared second-order nonlinear processes, the PNN model is implemented on optical, microwave, and acoustic-wave platforms without the need of fine tuning the parameters. Evaluated on a unified four-class classification task, the frequency-domain PNN achieves high inference accuracies of 97.6% in optics, 98.4% in electronics, and 98.2% in mechanics. Further, we systematically compare related performance metrics across these three distinct physical domains, paving the way to a universal neural network across different physical platforms.

</details>


### [134] [Independent Amplitude and Phase Modulation in Active Transmissive Metasurfaces via Complex Permittivity Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01085)
*Christopher M. Yi,Ruzan Sokhoyan,Juyoung Kim,Sungmin Hong,Jinwoo Song,Harry A. Atwater,Min Seok Jang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文理论提出一种中红外主动透射式超表面，通过Ge Mie谐振器与石墨烯条带集成，结合电栅控与Ge热光效应，实现独立振幅与相位调制，并演示了衍射效率超90%的光束偏转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动超表面是空间光调制器（SLM）的理想平台，可在亚波长尺度动态调控光的振幅与相位。透射式超表面便于与芯片级光源单片集成，是传统SLM的紧凑替代方案。然而，独立振幅与相位调制此前仅在主动反射式超表面中实现，透射式因设计挑战尚未达成。

Method: 提出由高Q值锗（Ge）长方体Mie谐振器与石墨烯条带集成的主动透射式超表面。利用石墨烯的带内与带间跃迁，通过静电栅控在同一器件中于一个频率实现透射率从近0%至5%的调制，于另一频率实现281°纯相位调制。为在同一工作波长同时实现两类调制，利用Ge的热光效应作为全局调谐机制，在光谱上对准谐振频率，从而在不同基础温度下分别实现透射率与纯相位调制。最终通过逐单元独立寻址实现光束偏转。

Result: 双机制架构实现约100%的透射率调制效率和282°的纯相位调制；通过逐单元独立寻址实现的束偏转器件相对衍射效率超过90%。

Conclusion: 该可独立寻址的主动超表面为紧凑、可动态重构的元光子器件开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Active metasurfaces are a promising platform for spatial light modulators (SLMs) in applications such as holography, beam shaping, and light detection and ranging (LiDAR), as they enable dynamic control over the amplitude and phase of light at subwavelength scales. Among these, transmissive metasurfaces offer a compact alternative to conventional SLMs, since they allow monolithic integration with chip-scale light sources. While independent amplitude and phase modulation have been previously demonstrated in active reflective metasurfaces, this important milestone has not been achieved in transmission due to the inherent challenges in designing active transmissive metasurfaces. Here, we theoretically demonstrate an active transmissive metasurface that enables independent amplitude and phase modulation in the mid-infrared. The proposed metasurface consists of high-Q cuboid germanium (Ge) Mie resonators integrated with graphene stripes. Utilizing the intraband and interband transitions of graphene, electrostatic gating enables modulation of the transmittance from near 0% to 5% at one spectral frequency and achieves 281° phase-only modulation at another frequency in the same device. To achieve both types of modulation at the same operating wavelength, we use the thermo-optic effect of Ge as a global tuning mechanism to spectrally align the resonant frequencies, enabling both transmittance and phase-only modulation at different base temperatures. The proposed dual-mechanism architecture achieves transmittance modulation efficiency of ~100% and 282° phase-only modulation. Finally, we demonstrate the efficacy of phase-only modulation by individually addressing each unit cell to realize a beam-steering device with relative diffraction efficiencies over 90%. Our individually addressable active metasurface opens a route toward compact, dynamically reconfigurable metaphotonic devices.

</details>


### [135] [Vector solitons in nonlinear lattices with competing SPM and XPM terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01212)
*Liangwei Zeng,Boris A. Malomed,Dumitru Mihalache,Xing Zhu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究证明，具有反相空间调制SPM与XPM系数且符号相反的双组分光子非线性晶格可支持基态与混合两类矢量孤子，并揭示了其结构与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在双组分光子非线性晶格中引入反相空间调制的自相位调制（SPM）与交叉相位调制（XPM）系数、且二者符号相反时，能否形成新型光学矢量孤子（VSs）并保持稳定。
