Method: 构建含反相空间调制SPM与XPM系数（符号相反）的双组分光子非线性晶格模型，求解其矢量孤子解，分别考察基态与混合两类VS的结构，并通过稳定性分析确定其稳定参数区间。

Result: 发现两类光学矢量孤子：基态VS由两个类高斯组分构成；混合VS由类高斯组分与偶极组分组合而成。两类VS总体上稳定，仅在传播常数较小时存在窄的失稳区间。

Conclusion: 反相空间调制且符号相反的SPM/XPM系数为矢量孤子的形成提供了有效机制，所得基态与混合VS在宽参数范围内稳定，仅在小传播常数处出现窄的不稳定窗口。

Abstract: We demonstrate that two-component photonic nonlinear lattices with opposite signs of the out-of-phase spatially modulated SPM and XPM coefficients can support two types of optical vector solitons (VSs), the fundamental and mixed ones. The fundamental VSs are composed of two Gaussian-like components, while the mixed ones combine Gaussian-like and dipole components. The structures and stability of both types of the VSs are investigated. The VSs are stable, except for narrow intervals at small values of the propagation constant.

</details>


### [136] [A Mid-Infrared Absorber Inspired by the Wing-Scale Photonic Structure of Troides magellanus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01274)
*Amandine Marchand,Kevin Delmote,Olivier Deparis,Sébastien R. Mouchet*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 受Troides magellanus蝴蝶翅鳞结构启发，通过几何缩放将可见光光学响应迁移至中红外，经数值模拟优化获得宽带中红外吸收器，吸收较平面钢参考提升391%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境与能源挑战推动了对高效能量回收与资源利用技术的需求。仿生光子结构为高效红外吸收器提供了新思路，但自然界结构的光学响应通常位于可见光波段，需通过结构缩放拓展至红外应用。

Method: 以Troides magellanus（麦哲伦鸟翼蝶）高吸收翅鳞为生物原型，提取其特征几何并进行尺度重设与适配，使光学响应从可见光迁移至中红外；采用数值模拟分析主要几何参数对光谱吸收率的影响，并据此确定优化构型。

Result: 优化结构相较平面钢参考实现391%的吸收增强。性能提升归因于优化后的高长径比几何形貌，其增强了多重散射与光捕获，从而扩大了有效光-物质相互作用面积。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仿生几何缩放是设计高效红外吸收器并调控其光谱响应的有效策略，可服务于热辐射管理与红外光子应用。

Abstract: Growing environmental and energy-related challenges have intensified the search for technologies capable of improving energy recovery and resource efficiency. Bioinspired photonic structures offer promising opportunities for the development of efficient infrared absorbers by exploiting optical mechanisms found in natural systems. In this work, we present a bioinspired approach for the design and optimization of a broadband mid-infrared absorber based on the highly absorbing wing scales of the Troides magellanus Magellan birdwing butterfly. The characteristic geometry of the biological structure is rescaled and adapted to shift its optical response from the visible to the infrared spectral range. Numerical simulations are used to investigate the influence of the main geometrical parameters on the spectral absorptance and to identify an optimized configuration. The optimized structure achieves an absorption enhancement of 391% compared with a flat steel reference. The improved optical response is attributed to the optimized high-aspect-ratio geometry, which enhances multiple scattering and light trapping, thereby increasing the effective light-matter interaction area. These results demonstrate the potential of bioinspired geometric scaling as an effective strategy for engineering efficient infrared absorbers and tailoring their spectral response for thermal radiation management and infrared photonic applications.

</details>


### [137] [Inverted Leakage Radiation Microscopy for measurement of nonreciprocity induced by a plasmonic metasurface with false chirality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01374)
*Ahmed Lafeef Ettapuram Naduvilepurayil,Sahil Sahoo,Yuri Gorodetski*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文实验证明了在等离激元超表面中探测非互易性的方法，并利用倒置漏辐射显微镜（ILRM）确定其方向，发现非互易性与介质的"假手性"相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易性（光在相反方向传播时经历不同的光学响应）是信号处理、降噪、单向传播和传感等多种应用的根源，因此需要一种紧凑且通用的方法来探测非互易性及其方向。

Method: 采用倒置漏辐射显微镜（Inverted Leakage Radiation Microscopy, ILRM）装置；该装置等价于经典萨格纳克环（Sagnac loop），在像平面上产生干涉图样；通过分析所得图像的拓扑序（topological order）来探测非互易性，并将其与介质的假手性（false chirality）相联系。

Result: 实验成功探测到等离激元超表面中的非互易性，并通过干涉图样的拓扑序确定了非互易性的方向；证实非互易性源于介质的假手性。

Conclusion: 该方案为探测等离激元学中的假手性提供了一种紧凑而通用的方法，并有望在纳米光子学、化学及应用物理等领域获得广泛应用。

Abstract: A system has a nonreciprocity when the light propagating through it in opposite directions encounters different optical adventures. This effect lies in the origin of various applications, including signal processing, noise reduction, unidirectional propagation, and sensing. Here, we experimentally demonstrate the detection of nonreciprocity in plasmonic metasurfaces and a method to determine its direction. We use the Inverted Leakage Radiation Microscopy setup to show that the non- reciprocity is linked to a false chirality of the medium. The setup behaves as an equivalent of the classical Sagnac loop providing an interference pattern at the image plane. The non-reciprocity is then detected by examining the topological order of the resulting image. Our system represents a compact and versatile method to detect false chirality in plasmonics and suggests a variety of potential areas of implementation in nanophotonics, chemistry and applied physics.

</details>


### [138] [Stochastic switching in quadrupole-trap-based levitodynamic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01387)
*Vadim Rybin,Semyon Rudyi,Dmitrii Shcherbinin*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了大气压下四极阱中带电微粒在主参数共振附近的随机切换动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究参数共振条件下带电微粒在线性与非线性运动区制间的随机切换机制，并评估其在精密检测中的潜在价值。

Method: 基于热噪声激活逃逸与耗散回归之间的平衡建立理论模型，将切换过程描述为有效Gibbs分布，并引入有效势垒与有效系统温度。

Result: 切换行为服从有效Gibbs分布；有效势垒依赖粒子质量与电荷；热噪声反而揭示而非掩盖该转变，呈指数依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该指数依赖可为噪声感知型精密检测方案提供基础，且系统可作为研究非平衡参数系统激活动力学的高度可调平台。

Abstract: We have theoretically investigated the stochastic switching dynamics of a charged microparticle in a quadrupole trap near the principal parametric resonance at atmospheric pressure. We show that this behavior arises from a balance of thermal noise-activated escape and dissipative return between linear and nonlinear dynamical regimes of the particle motion. The switching follows an effective Gibbs distribution with an effective potential barrier between the regimes and effective system temperature, which are both experimentally accessible. Because the effective barrier depends on particle mass and charge, and since thermal noise reveals rather than obscures the transition, this exponential dependence could provide the foundation to precision noise-aware detection schemes. The system further offers a highly adjustable platform for studying activation dynamics in non-equilibrium parametric systems.

</details>


### [139] [Engineering photonic crystal slab modes for strong exciton-photon coupling and polariton dispersion control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01473)
*Natalia Salakhova,Andrey Demenev,Vladimir Kulakovskii,Nikolay Gippius*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文数值研究了周期加倍Si3N4光子晶体板与hBN封装MoSe2单层的杂化结构中的激子-光子耦合，通过hBN厚度调控场分布与模式杂化，实现Rabi分裂从约3-4 meV增强至8.5 meV，并揭示有限动量极化激元的形成机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种可灵活调控辐射耦合、激子-光子空间重叠及极化激元色散的平台，以研究过渡金属硫族化物单层杂化结构中的光-物质相互作用。周期加倍光子晶体板可将准导模折入光锥并产生辐射性质不同的光子支，但其与hBN封装TMD单层耦合的调控机制尚待阐明。

Method: 构建周期加倍Si3N4光子晶体板与hBN封装MoSe2单层的杂化结构进行数值模拟。周期加倍使准导模折入光锥，产生光谱分离且辐射特性受加倍原胞对称性控制的光子支，在正入射下形成一个亮模和一个BIC-like模。以hBN厚度作为独立参数调控垂直场分布，比较亮模与BIC-like配置，并分析有限动量下折叠TE-like模的杂化及附加光子带隙。

Result: 正入射下形成一个亮模和一个BIC-like模。增大hBN厚度使共振场向MoSe2单层再分布，Rabi分裂由约3-4 meV增至8.5 meV，耦合强度由约1.5-1.7 meV增至4.3-4.4 meV。亮模与BIC-like配置对比表明，品质因子主要决定辐射可见度与线宽，而耦合强度主要由单层处场幅值决定。hBN层调控有限动量下折叠TE-like模的杂化，产生可由hBN厚度调节的附加光子带隙，并在约113 nm处闭合。杂化结构中观察到有限动量激子-极化激元态，依hBN厚度不同，其形成可源于与单一孤立光子支或与两个近简并光子模的耦合。

Conclusion: 周期加倍光子晶体板可作为灵活平台，用于调控基于TMD单层杂化结构中的辐射耦合、激子-光子重叠及极化激元色散。

Abstract: We numerically study exciton-photon coupling in a hybrid structure composed of a period-doubled Si3N4 photonic crystal slab and an hBN-encapsulated MoSe2 monolayer. Period doubling folds quasi-guided modes into the light cone and produces spectrally separated photonic branches whose radiative character is controlled by the symmetry of the doubled unit cell, resulting in one bright and one bound state in the continuum (BIC)-like mode at normal incidence. We then use the hBN thickness as an independent tuning parameter for the vertical field profile. Increasing the hBN thickness redistributes the resonant field toward the MoSe2 monolayer and enhances the Rabi splitting from about 3-4 meV to 8.5 meV, corresponding to an increase in the coupling strength from approximately 1.5-1.7 meV to 4.3-4.4 meV. Comparison between bright and BIC-like configurations shows that the quality factor primarily determines radiative visibility and linewidth, whereas the coupling strength is mainly governed by the field amplitude at the monolayer. We further show that the hBN layer controls the hybridization of folded TE-like modes at finite momentum, leading to an additional photonic gap whose size can be tuned by the hBN thickness and closes at an hBN thickness of approximately 113 nm. In the hybrid structure, we observe finite-momentum exciton-polariton states that, depending on the hBN thickness, form either through coupling to an isolated photonic branch or through coupling to two nearly degenerate photonic modes. These results identify period-doubled photonic crystal slabs as a flexible platform for controlling radiative coupling, exciton-photon overlap, and polariton dispersion in hybrid structures based on transition-metal dichalcogenide monolayers.

</details>


### [140] [Inherent Turbulence Immunity of Vector Vortex Beams in Free Space Quantum Key Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01523)
*Behnam Talari,Rouhollah Karimzadeh*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文提出以偏振-OAM混合纠缠的矢量涡旋光束(VVB)编码，利用大气湍流对正交圆偏振分量的共模对称耦合，在无硬件补偿条件下实现湍流免疫的高容量自由空间QKD，将渐近QBER从42.0%降至4.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OAM复用提供无限维离散Hilbert空间，适合高容量自由空间QKD；但纯标量空间模式(|ℓ|≥1)在陆地大气湍流中发生严重退相干：湍流折射率涡旋撕裂高阶涡旋奇点，导致相邻拓扑信道间灾难性串扰，使QBER迅速超过个体克隆攻击的11%无条件安全阈值。因此需要一种无需主动自适应光学的内在抗湍流编码方案。

Method: 采用偏振-OAM混合纠缠的矢量涡旋光束(VVB)编码。理论依据是陆地空气光学各向异性极小(Δn<10⁻⁹)，折射率起伏对正交圆偏振模式对称耦合，表现为相同的共模标量相位屏，在相对偏振-相位自由度上相互抵消。数值上，将模式场通过改进的功率谱相位屏进行传播仿真，湍流强度范围D/r₀=0至3.0。

Result: 在D/r₀=0至3.0的湍流强度范围内，VVB编码协议将渐近QBER从42.0%抑制至4.8%，误码抑制因子约11.6，且无需主动自适应光学或变形镜等硬件。

Conclusion: VVB编码通过相对偏振-相位自由度对共模湍流相位屏的天然抵消，提供内在、免硬件的湍流免疫能力，使自由空间OAM-QKD在高湍流下仍远低于11%安全阈值，为高容量自由空间量子通信提供了实用化路径。

Abstract: Orbital angular momentum (OAM) multiplexing provides an infinite-dimensional discrete Hilbert space ideally suited for high-capacity free-space quantum key distribution (QKD). Nevertheless, pure scalar spatial modes carrying topological charge ($|\ell| \ge 1$) undergo severe decoherence when transmitted through terrestrial atmospheric turbulence. Turbulent refractive-index eddies split high-order vortex singularities, induce catastrophic intermodal crosstalk across adjacent topological channels, and rapidly drive the quantum bit error rate (QBER) well above the unconditional 11% security threshold associated with individual cloning attacks. Here, we demonstrate that hybrid polarization-OAM entangled states, known as vector vortex beams (VVBs), provide intrinsic, hardware-free immunity against turbulent perturbations. Because the optical anisotropy of terrestrial air is exceedingly small ($Δn < 10^{-9}$), refractive-index fluctuations couple symmetrically to orthogonal circular polarization modes as an identical common-mode scalar phase screen that cancels in the relative polarization-phase degree of freedom. By numerically propagating modal fields through modified power-spectral phase screens over turbulence strengths ranging from $D/r_0 = 0$ to $3.0$, we show that the VVB encoding protocol suppresses the asymptotic QBER from 42.0% to 4.8%, yielding an error-suppression factor of approximately 11.6 without requiring active adaptive optics or deformable mirrors.

</details>


### [141] [Measuring Geometric Phase based on Indefinite Causal Order in a Sagnac Interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01540)
*Lucas Marques Fagundes,Finlay Campbell,Richard Aguiar Maduro,Renné Medeiros de Araújo,Stephen M. Barnett,Sarah Croke,Sonja Franke-Arnold*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文揭示Sagnac干涉仪的逆向光路特性可实现非确定因果序的经典类比，并通过单次测量识别两个非对易偏振操作对应的几何相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sagnac干涉仪因共路噪声抵消而具备高稳定性，且对旋转敏感，是激光陀螺仪的基础。但作者关注其较少被探索的特性：顺、逆时针光分量以相反顺序经过光学元件，这为研究"非确定因果序"（事件顺序不固定）的经典实现提供了机会。

Method: 利用Sagnac干涉仪中顺、逆时针传播光经过光学元件顺序相反的特点，构建非确定因果序的经典实现方案，并通过单次测量提取两个非对易偏振操作所关联的几何相位。

Result: 实现了对两个非对易偏振操作几何相位的单次测量，给出非确定因果序的几何图示；可望应用于快速测定偏振操控或手性介质中的光学活性。

Conclusion: Sagnac干涉仪的逆向光路提供了非确定因果序的经典实现，其首要价值在于给出非确定因果序的几何直观图示，同时具备偏振操控与手性介质光学活性快速测量的潜在应用。

Abstract: Sagnac interferometers are important tools for precision measurements. Cancellation of common-path noise ensures a high degree of stability against external perturbations, while their sensitivity to rotations provides the basis of laser gyroscopes. Here we highlight another, less explored feature of Sagnac interferometers: the fact that optical elements are traversed in reverse order for clockwise and counterclockwise propagating light components. This provides the opportunity to explore a classical realization of indefinite causal order, where the order of events within a sequence is not fixed. We explore this concept to identify the geometric phase associated with two non-commuting polarization operations with a single measurement. This idea may have applications for the rapid determination of polarization manipulations or optical activity within chiral media, but foremost it provides a geometric illustration of indefinite causal order.

</details>


### [142] [Digitally enhanced Multi-wavelength Stabilization using a Passive Fiber Frequency Reference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01575)
*Hilma Karlsson,Vaishali Adya,Robert L. Ward,Chathura P. Bandutunga*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出基于编码复用的无源光纤干涉仪方案，实现相距85 GHz的两台激光器共用单一无源参考的频率稳定与传递，闭环外频率噪声低于亚kHz/√Hz，1 s差分分数稳定度达7×10⁻¹³，并给出向更多激光器扩展的架构框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光间高稳定频率稳定对精密计量、频率传递和量子通信等应用至关重要。传统多光源稳定通常依赖光学频率梳，存在系统复杂、成本高、扩展性受限等问题，因此需要一种无需光频梳、可扩展且频率灵活的多光源稳定方案。

Method: 采用基于编码复用的无源光纤干涉仪作为共享参考，对频率间隔85 GHz的两台激光器同时进行频率稳定与传递；通过闭环外（out-of-loop）测量表征传递后的频率噪声，并评估残余长度噪声耦合；此外提出将该架构扩展至更多激光器的框架。

Result: 在40 mHz以上傅里叶频率处，传递频率噪声上界为亚kHz/√Hz；1 s处差分分数稳定度为7×10⁻¹³；实现了激光的连续可调谐并表征了残余长度噪声耦合；所提框架可支持更多激光器数量的扩展。

Conclusion: 该工作证明利用编码复用的单一无源光纤参考即可实现多激光器高稳定频率稳定与传递，无需光学频率梳，为可扩展、频率灵活的多光源稳定提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: High-stability frequency stabilization between lasers is essential for applications such as precision metrology, frequency dissemination, and quantum communications. We demonstrate the use of code-based multiplexing of a passive fiber-based interferometer for frequency stabilization and transfer between lasers separated by 85 GHz in frequency using a single shared passive reference. Out-of-loop characterization places an upper bound of sub-kHz/$\sqrt{\text{Hz}}$ on the transferred frequency noise at Fourier frequencies above 40 mHz, with a differential fractional stability of $7\times10^{-13}$ at 1 second. We also demonstrate continuous laser tunability and characterize the residual length noise coupling. Finally, we provide a framework for scaling the architecture to higher laser counts, offering a pathway to scalable, frequency agile stabilization of multiple light sources without the overhead of an optical frequency comb.

</details>


### [143] [Programmable nonlinearity within nanophotonic waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02024)
*Benjamin A. Ash,Ryotatsu Yanagimoto,Mandar M. Sohoni,Yang Xu,Yiqi Zhao,Martin M. Stein,Fan O. Wu,Marc Jankowski,Logan G. Wright,Tatsuhiro Onodera,Peter L. McMahon*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过在纳米光子波导内构建可编程χ⁽²⁾全息图，实现可重构准相位匹配，可在器件制备后约1秒内更新，并结合原位闭环优化补偿相位失配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纳米光子学中的相位匹配依赖固定几何电极的极化等纳米加工手段，存在两个缺陷：器件光学功能在制造时即被固定，且制造缺陷会降低器件性能。

Method: 将结构化光照明投影到光电导电极上，产生电场诱导χ⁽²⁾的空间图案，从而对三波混频过程施加光谱、模式与偏振控制；并利用闭环原位优化补偿复杂波导结构中的相位失配。

Result: 实现了约1秒内可更新的可重构准相位匹配；在宽度调制波导及长度超过10 cm的螺旋波导中补偿相位失配；实现多种横模的直接色散测量与片上波前相位层析等原位诊断功能。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建级联非线性纳米光子系统提供了可能，既可规避制造差异导致的良率问题，又能保留刻蚀波导在光场限制与色散工程方面的优势。

Abstract: Nonlinear nanophotonics enables the engineering of optical functions using light-matter interactions in nanoscale structures with carefully designed geometries. Phase matching in nonlinear optics is typically essential for realizing high efficiency for desired processes and engineering of sophisticated functions, but it can be challenging to reliably achieve in nanophotonic waveguides. Conventionally, phase matching in nanophotonics is achieved through nanofabrication, e.g., by poling a device's nonlinear material using electrodes with fixed geometry. This approach has two drawbacks: the optical functions a device can perform are fixed at the time of fabrication, and the device's performance can be degraded by fabrication imperfections. Here, we circumvent these limitations by engineering programmable $χ^{(2)}$ holograms inside nanophotonic waveguides, enabling reconfigurable quasi-phase matching that can be updated after device fabrication in approximately one second. By projecting structured optical illumination onto photoconductive electrodes, we generated spatial patterns of electric-field-induced $χ^{(2)}$ to exert spectral, modal, and polarization control over three-wave mixing processes. This programmability also allowed us to employ closed-loop in situ optimization to compensate for phase mismatch in complex waveguide structures, such as width-modulated waveguides and spiral waveguides exceeding 10 cm in length. Furthermore, we show that programmable nonlinearity enables powerful in situ diagnostics, including direct dispersion measurements of various transverse modes and on-chip wavefront phase tomography. Our work opens up the possibility of building cascaded nonlinear nanophotonic systems where yield issues due to fabrication variations can be sidestepped while retaining the benefits of etched waveguides such as confinement and dispersion engineering.

</details>


### [144] [Thin-capping layer epitaxial quantum dots for near-field quantum photonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02080)
*Yuting Guo,Jonathan Bar-David,Pasquale Cilibrizzi,Sung-Yul L. Park,Jin Dong Song,Luca Sapienza*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文通过将InAs/GaAs量子点的GaAs盖层减薄至10 nm，在保持激子寿命不变的前提下,将发射线宽降低至此前报道的1/5，证明近表面量子点仍能维持高光学质量，为近场光-物质相互作用研究奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外延量子点虽为优质量子光源，但通常需超过50 nm的厚盖层以隔离表面态，这阻碍了等离激元等近场效应的应用——近场耦合要求发射体与金属结构相距仅数十纳米甚至更近。因此需要验证减薄盖层后量子点能否仍保持高光学质量。

Method: 生长了GaAs盖层厚度分别为10 nm、20 nm及标准95 nm的InAs/GaAs量子点，并对其进行光学表征，比较不同盖层厚度下的发射线宽与激子寿命。

Result: 观察到发射线宽比此前报道小至多5倍，且激子寿命未受扰动，与标准厚盖层样品相当。

Conclusion: 外延量子点即使距表面较近，仍可保持高光学质量，从而为利用相干且稳定发射体探索近场光-物质相互作用、应用于量子技术提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Epitaxial quantum dots (QDs) are widely recognised as one of the best quantum light sources, given their good stability, brightness, quantum efficiency and coherence. To reach such properties, QDs are protected from potentially detrimental surface states by a relatively thick capping layer, typically exceeding 50nm. This prevents the implementation of near-field effects, like plasmonic-based ones, that can dramatically increase the light-matter interaction, since the control of the spontaneous emission dynamics and directionality of the emission can only occur if the emitter is in close proximity (typically tens of nanometers or less) from the metallic structures. In this work, we report the growth and optical characterisation of InAs/GaAs QDs with GaAs capping layer thickness of 10nm, 20nm, and a more standard 95nm. Remarkably, we observe emission linewidths up to 5 times smaller than previously reported, with unperturbed excitonic lifetimes. These results demonstrate that the epitaxial QD's high optical quality can be maintained even when the emitters are at reduced distances from the surface, opening the path for the exploration of near-field light-matter interactions with coherent and stable emitters, suitable for quantum technology applications.

</details>

### [145] [Tunable flat bands and their signatures in electronic specific heat of an Aharonov-Bohm triangular quantum network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00273)
*Sanchita Nandi,Santanu K. Maiti*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究由三角形环构成的紧束缚量子网络的热响应，发现平带形成会在电子比热中留下可辨识特征，为探测平带提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环状结构量子网络的电子输运已被广泛研究，但其热响应相对缺乏关注；同时，平带的识别通常依赖能谱或输运测量，作者希望探索热学手段能否作为补充探测方式。

Method: 构建由有限个三角plaquette通过单键连接组成的紧束缚量子网络，每个三角形环穿透Aharonov-Bohm磁通φ；通过解析推导得到某一能带与电子动量无关（即平带）的条件，并计算电子比热（ESH）随温度、磁通及紧束缚参数的变化。

Result: 非均匀格点配位、跳跃不对称与量子干涉共同产生色散能带与完全平坦能带；给出了能带动量无关的解析条件，并表明AB磁通φ可直接调节平带位置；平带位置在电子比热中表现为显著特征，从而建立了平带形成与热响应之间的直接联系。

Conclusion: 电子比热可作为识别动量无关能级（平带）的替代手段，与解析条件和能量色散结果一致；AB磁通可选择性调控电子比热。该方法简单互补，并可推广至其他支持平带的简单或复杂量子网络。

Abstract: Quantum networks composed of loop-like structures provide a rich platform for exploring electronic transport phenomena and have been widely studied in various contexts. However, their thermal response remains relatively unexplored, motivating us to investigate it in the present work. We consider a tight-binding (TB) quantum network composed of a finite number of triangular plaquettes, where neighboring plaquettes are connected through single bonds and each triangular loop is threaded by an Aharonov-Bohm (AB) flux $φ$. The interplay of nonuniform site coordination, hopping asymmetry, and quantum interference gives rise to both dispersive and completely flat energy levels. We derive an analytical condition involving the TB parameters under which one of the energy branches becomes independent of the electronic momentum. We further show that the AB flux $φ$ provides a direct means of tuning the position of the flat band. The characteristic features of the energy spectrum associated with this quantum geometry are reflected in the thermal response, which we investigate through the electronic specific heat (ESH). In particular, we demonstrate that the position of the flat band manifests itself as a distinct feature in the ESH, establishing a direct connection between the flat-band formation and the thermal response of the system. Thus, the ESH provides an alternative route for identifying the presence of a flat band, consistent with the analytical condition and the corresponding energy dispersion. Moreover, the AB flux allows the ESH to be selectively regulated through the tuning of the flat-band position. The proposed approach provides a simple complementary means of identifying momentum-independent energy levels and may be extended to other simple and complex quantum networks supporting flat bands.

</details>


### [146] [Interior contacts in a narrow quantum Hall bar: a two-dimensional self-consistent screening calculation of the current distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00326)
*A. Siddiki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在六端GaAs/AlGaAs霍尔条中，采用屏蔽理论下二维自洽Thomas-Fermi-Poisson计算，研究了内部接触在ν=2平台随磁场降低经历电连接、浮空和隔离三个区间，隔离电阻随温度与朗道能级展宽变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解释Kendirlik等人内部接触实验，在整数量子霍尔效应屏蔽理论框架下理解内部接触与体态/边缘态之间的连接及电阻行为。

Method: 在完整器件平面（宽3微米霍尔条、两个经空气桥连接的方形内部接触、探测臂及耗尽区）上自洽求解Thomas-Fermi-Poisson方程，得到电子密度、局域填充因子和电流分布；电流由填充因子依赖电导率张量的局域欧姆定律给出，分别考虑源漏和内部接触激发。

Result: 跨越ν=2平台时内部接触随磁场降低呈现三种区间：可压缩体态中与电流路径电连接、完全不可压缩体态中浮空于霍尔电势、被围绕接触的闭合不可压缩环与体态和边缘条隔离；浮空和隔离区间两端电阻上升数个量级。隔离区间位于霍尔平台内并在低场边缘前结束，其高场端由不可压缩体态消失决定，低场端随温度和朗道能级展宽缩小；内部接触与外围探针的电阻接近量子化霍尔电阻的一半。

Conclusion: 不可压缩区拓扑及隔离区间边界对局域电导正则化、网格和中等无序不敏感，但隔离电阻量值敏感。

Abstract: We present fully two-dimensional self-consistent calculations of the electron density, local filling factor and current distribution in a six-terminal GaAs/AlGaAs Hall bar of width W = 3 micrometers with two additional square contacts, A and B, placed on the channel axis and connected to the outside by air bridges, following the interior-contact experiment of Kendirlik et al. [Nat. Commun. 8, 14082 (2017)]. Within the screening theory of the integer quantized Hall effect, the Thomas-Fermi-Poisson equations are solved on the full device plane, including the probe arms and the depletion regions around the interior contacts, and the current is obtained from a local Ohm's law with a filling-factor dependent conductivity tensor, for source-drain and interior-contact excitation separately. Across the nu = 2 plateau the interior contacts pass through three regimes as the field is lowered: galvanically connected to the current path in a compressible bulk, floating in the Hall potential of a fully incompressible bulk, and isolated from both the bulk and the edge strips by closed incompressible rings encircling each contact. In the floating and isolated regimes the two-terminal resistance between the interior contacts rises by orders of magnitude. The isolation interval lies within the Hall plateau and ends before its low-field edge; its high-field end is sharply set by the disappearance of the incompressible bulk, while its low-field end shrinks with temperature and Landau-level broadening. The resistance between an interior contact and a perimeter probe approaches one half of the quantized Hall resistance across the plateau. The topology of the incompressible regions and the boundaries of the isolation interval are insensitive to the regularization of the local conductivity, to the grid and to moderate disorder, whereas the magnitude of the isolation resistance is not.

</details>


### [147] [From Quantized Hall Plateaus to Topological Surfaces: Quantum Capacitance as a Unifying Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00336)
*A. Siddiki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文论证量子电容（quantum capacitance）是探测拓扑边界态密度（density of states）的关键静电手段，将量子霍尔效应、拓扑绝缘体和量子电容这三个通常分开讨论的主题用"体拓扑决定边界态、量子电容探测态密度"这一主线统一起来，并指出自洽屏蔽理论揭示的绝缘体假设的局域场依赖性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子霍尔效应、拓扑绝缘体与量子电容通常被当作彼此独立的课题处理，但二者共享一条主线：体拓扑（bulk topology）决定了受保护的边界态（protected boundary state），而量子电容是少数能够探测态密度（DOS）的静电方法之一。作者希望厘清这一内在联系，并重新审视量子电容在这些现象中的地位。

Method: 本文属于综述/理论评论性工作，采用概念梳理与文献综合的方法：一方面追溯整数量子霍尔效应中量子化边界输运的拓扑起源（由陈数 Chern number 描述），及其向零磁场下 Z2 拓扑绝缘体（螺旋边缘态与狄拉克表面态）的推广；另一方面梳理量子电容从二维电子气（2DEG）研究工具发展为映射朗道能级（Landau level）态密度与拓扑表面态线性狄拉克态密度的关键技术；同时引入自洽屏蔽理论（self-consistent screening theory）与局域量子电容测量结果进行对照分析。

Result: 量子电容被确立为探测量子霍尔效应朗道能级态密度、以及拓扑表面态线性狄拉克态密度的重要手段。自洽屏蔽理论表明，量子霍尔体态会分裂为可压缩区（compressible regions）与不可压缩区（incompressible regions），且这些区域随磁场变化而移动。局域量子电容测量进一步证实：在量子化平台（quantised plateau）上，体态并非均匀绝缘，尽管平台本身仍保持量子化。

Conclusion: 作者主张量子电容并非上述现象的次要特征，而是窥视"拓扑所强加的边界态密度"最清晰的窗口之一；它还揭示了量子霍尔效应与拓扑绝缘体类比背后所依赖的"体态绝缘"假设具有局域性和场依赖性，从而需要被更细致地修正。

Abstract: The quantum Hall effect, topological insulators and quantum capacitance are usually treated as distinct topics, yet they share a common thread. Bulk topology determines a protected boundary state, and quantum capacitance is one of the few electrostatic methods able to detect the density of states. This paper examines that connection. The integer quantum Hall effect showed that quantised boundary transport has a topological origin given by the Chern number, later extended to zero field in Z2 topological insulators with helical edge and Dirac surface states. Quantum capacitance, developed for two-dimensional electron gases, has since become a key technique for mapping the Landau level density of states underlying the quantum Hall effect and the linear Dirac density of states of topological surface states. This picture is complicated by self-consistent screening theory, which shows that the quantum Hall bulk splits into compressible and incompressible regions that shift with field. Local quantum capacitance measurements confirm that the bulk is not uniformly insulating across a quantised plateau, even though the plateau stays quantised. The paper argues that quantum capacitance is not a secondary feature of these phenomena but one of the clearest windows into the boundary density of states that topology imposes, and that it reveals the local, field-dependent nature of the insulating bulk assumption underlying the analogy between the quantum Hall effect and topological insulators.

</details>


### [148] [Strongly coupled ultracompressed self-cavity plasmon polaritons at millikelvin temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00473)
*Luojia Zhang,Andrew T. Pierce,Xuepeng Wang,Simon Reinhardt,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Kin Fai Mak,Jie Shan,Debanjan Chowdhury,Valla Fatemi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于超导层的自腔结构平台，将电磁体积压缩约10^7倍，并结合石墨烯层在0.04 THz观测到欠阻尼腔等离激元极化激元及其与腔模的避免交叉，同时开发了兼容毫开尔文的亚太赫兹光谱仪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德瓦尔斯异质结构具有从亚太赫兹到太赫兹的自腔共振，可用于研究腔修饰的物相，但其光谱测量需要在毫开尔文温度下具备高频率分辨率，现有方法极难实现，因此需要新的探测平台。

Method: 利用超导层构成自腔结构，实现约10^7量级的电磁体积压缩；集成石墨烯层以引入腔等离激元极化激元；并搭建基于超导电路的、兼容毫开尔文温度的亚太赫兹光谱仪进行光谱测量。

Result: 在低至0.04 THz的频率下观测到欠阻尼腔等离激元极化激元，并观察到其与腔模之间的避免交叉现象。

Conclusion: 该平台与光谱技术为在毫开尔文温度下同时对腔-物质杂化系统进行光谱与输运测量开辟了道路。

Abstract: Van der Waals heterostructures exhibit self-cavity resonances from sub-terahertz to terahertz frequencies and thus offer an attractive route toward realizing cavity-modified phases of matter. Spectroscopy of such systems calls for high frequency resolution at millikelvin temperatures, which is extremely challenging with existing methods. Here we demonstrate a new platform for realizing and probing such systems. Superconducting layers define self-cavity structures with electromagnetic volume compression of order $10^7$. With an integrated graphene layer, we observe underdamped cavity plasmon polaritons at frequencies as low as 0.04 THz and which exhibit avoided crossings with cavity modes. To measure such spectra, we deploy a millikelvin-compatible sub-THz spectrometer based on a superconducting circuit. This work paves the way for simultaneous spectroscopy and transport measurements of cavity-matter hybrid systems at millikelvin temperatures.

</details>


### [149] [Algebraic Relaxation of Strong-to-Weak Symmetry-Breaking Order via Charge-Sector Broadening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00474)
*Zi-Xi Fang,Kai Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究强-弱自发对称破缺（SW-SSB）在强对称性被局域电荷交换破坏的Lindblad动力学下的衰减行为：离散Z₂对称呈指数衰减，连续U(1)对称呈代数慢弛豫，并给出两阶段动力学标度及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SW-SSB是混合量子态的独特现象，具有与强对称性相关的非线性长程秩序。但在实际开放量子系统中，强对称性宏观上脆弱，会因与环境局域电荷交换而退化为弱对称性。作者旨在探究这种强对称破缺的Liouvillian动力学如何影响SW-SSB秩序的衰减。

Method: 采用Liouvillian动力学框架，分别研究离散Z₂对称与连续U(1)对称下的SW-SSB衰减。对Z₂引入局域对称破缺微扰；对U(1)分析局域电荷交换导致的对称电荷分布展宽，并建立关联Rényi-1长程关联的严格代数下界，结合热力学极限下的动力学标度分析。数值上采用具有z=2与z=4的费米子链进行验证。

Result: 对离散Z₂对称，局域破缺微扰导致Rényi-1长程关联子指数衰减，揭示离散SW-SSB秩序的脆弱性；对连续U(1)对称，局域电荷交换使弛豫显著变慢并呈代数形式，作者给出严格的代数下界证明该慢弛豫的鲁棒性。热力学极限下发现两阶段动力学标度：早期R̃₁∼t⁻¹，晚期R̃₁∼t⁻¹ᐟᶻ，z由底层流体动力学决定。费米子链数值结果（z=2和z=4）与理论预测高度一致。

Conclusion: 强对称性的宏观脆弱性使SW-SSB秩序的衰减强烈依赖对称类型：离散对称指数脆弱，连续对称因电荷分布的缓慢展宽而呈鲁棒代数弛豫，并具有由流体动力学控制的普适两阶段标度，数值验证支持该理论框架。

Abstract: Strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SW-SSB) is a distinctive phenomenon of mixed quantum states, characterized by nonlinear long-range order associated with strong symmetry. In realistic open quantum systems, however, strong symmetry is macroscopically fragile and can be explicitly reduced to weak symmetry through local charge exchange with the environment. In this paper, we investigate how the SW-SSB order decays under such strong-symmetry-breaking Liouvillian dynamics. For discrete $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry, a local symmetry-breaking perturbation drives an exponential decay of the Rényi-1 long-range correlator, revealing the fragility of discrete SW-SSB order. By contrast, for continuous $U(1)$ symmetry, local charge exchange broadens the distribution over symmetry-charge sectors and leads to parametrically slower algebraic relaxation. We establish a rigorous algebraic lower bound on the connected Rényi-1 long-range correlation, demonstrating the robustness of this slow relaxation. In the thermodynamic limit, we further uncover a two-stage dynamical scaling: $\tilde{R}_1\sim t^{-1}$ at early times and $\tilde{R}_1\sim t^{-1/z}$ at long times, with $z$ set by the underlying hydrodynamic dynamics. As a numerical verification, we consider fermionic chains with $z=2$ and $z=4$, which are excellent agreement with the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [150] [How Superradiance Survives Molecular Noise inside Virus Capsids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00739)
*Tianran Li,Irina Tsvetkova,Matthew Brauser,Amala Raj,Philip Shushkov,Bogdan Dragnea*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将激发态有机染料共价结合到二十面体病毒衣壳上，实验证实其存在欠阻尼低频类声子振荡并调制染料激发态响应，理论建模表明这种衣壳运动可促进超辐射发射所需的集体极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室温下有机染料-病毒衣壳体系能自发同步并发射超辐射光，但该现象在碰撞退相干和非均匀展宽存在下仍能发生的机制尚不清楚；在易涨落的分子体系中，电子-振动耦合被认为起核心作用，需要厘清衣壳声子型模式是否参与从非相关发射到合作发射的转变。

Method: 实验上探测超辐射活性染料标记衣壳中是否存在欠阻尼的类声子低频模式；理论上建模该类衣壳运动对从非相关发射向合作发射转变的影响。

Result: 具有超辐射活性的染料标记衣壳表现出欠阻尼低频振荡，该振荡可调制染料的激发态响应；模型显示这种衣壳运动能够促进超辐射发射所需的集体极化。

Conclusion: 病毒衣壳的欠阻尼低频类声子运动通过调制染料激发态响应，为室温超辐射提供了促进集体极化的机制，电子-振动耦合在分子基体系的合作发射中起关键作用。

Abstract: Excited organic dyes chemically bound to an icosahedral virus shell can spontaneously synchronize and emit a superradiant burst of light. The mechanism that allows it to occur at room temperature, despite collisional dephasing and inhomogeneous broadening, is not understood. Few other known low-dimensional condensed matter systems exhibit room-temperature superradiance (SR). In the fluctuation-prone environment of molecular-based systems, electron--vibrational coupling has been proposed to play a central role. Here, we addressed experimentally the existence of underdamped virus capsid phonon-like modes and theoretically their involvement in the transition from uncorrelated to cooperative emission. We find that SR-active dye-labeled capsids exhibit underdamped low-frequency oscillations that modulate the dye excited-state response, and modeling shows how such capsid motion can promote the collective polarization required for superradiant emission.

</details>


### [151] [Tunable Electron Quantum Optics in Graphene Slit Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00768)
*Mohamed Amine Rhanbouri,Wojciech Julian Pasek,Abdelouahed El Fatimy*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 石墨烯狭缝结可作为集束电子束、相干干涉与自旋过滤于一体的紧凑器件几何，单层与双层体系均实现类电子光学束流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一种紧凑、可集成的几何结构，同时实现定向电子束形成、相干干涉调控和自旋过滤，以推动电子光学与自旋电子学器件的融合。

Method: 采用原子级紧束缚结合非平衡格林函数（NEGF）计算单层石墨烯狭缝结的输运；用傅里叶分析与谐波拟合表征磁通依赖振荡；用自洽平均场Hubbard方法研究自旋态；用四带连续模型进行双层石墨烯的能谷分辨计算。

Result: 单层石墨烯中三角度弯折在E≈2.7eV附近产生强定向电子束；边界反射将束流导向共同漏极，透射呈现h/e周期Aharonov-Bohm振荡，可由局域静电栅调控干涉相位；本征反铁磁基态透射自旋简并，左右对称性破缺（结构或静电）可实现自旋过滤；双层石墨烯在E=50meV出现类似束流形成。

Conclusion: 狭缝结是电子光学与自旋相关输运的统一几何平台，并激励进一步研究低能双层石墨烯实现方案。

Abstract: A graphene slit junction is investigated as a compact geometry for directional electron-beam formation, coherent interference, and symmetry-controlled spin filtering. Atomistic tight-binding calculations combined with the nonequilibrium Green's function formalism show that trigonal warping in monolayer graphene produces strongly directional electron beams near E~2.7eV. Boundary reflections redirect these beams toward a common drain, where the transmission exhibits magnetic flux-dependent oscillations consistent with an h/e Aharonov-Bohm period, whose modulation is characterized by Fourier analysis and harmonic fitting. A local electrostatic gate provides additional control of the interference phase. In separate self-consistent mean-field Hubbard calculations, the pristine antiferromagnetic ground state is shown to have spin-degenerate transmission, whereas structural or electrostatic breaking of the left-right symmetry enables spin filtering. Complementary valley-resolved calculations using a four-band continuum model demonstrate analogous beam formation in bilayer graphene at E = 50meV. The results identify the slit junction as a common geometry for electron-optical and spin-dependent transport and motivate further investigation of low-energy bilayer implementations.

</details>


### [152] [Hydrogen plasma-assisted atomic layer epitaxy of superconducting titanium nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00956)
*Yi-Hsun Chen,Yin-Chun Huang,Zachary Degnan,David Sommers,Kaijian Xing,Manjith Bose,Eduardo Solano,David Cortie,Michael Fuhrer,Julian A. Steele,Peter Jacobson,Miin-Jang Chen,Arkady Fedorov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作开发了氢等离子体辅助ALD，在c面蓝宝石上外延生长出结构有序的超导TiN薄膜，兼具低杂质、低无序与实用超导参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALD是半导体制造的成熟技术，具有精确、共形生长薄膜的优势，但用于超导量子电路时难以在低温下同时控制结晶性、化学计量比与界面无序，因此需要新的工艺路线。

Method: 发展氢等离子体辅助ALD，在每个沉积循环中引入氢等离子体步骤改性表面化学，抑制前驱体残留杂质；结合同步辐射X射线散射、X射线谱学与中子反射术表征薄膜结构与成分。

Result: TiN在c面蓝宝石上实现半共格外延，具有离散晶畴且长程结构无序极小；薄膜中氧相关无序可忽略、无显著氢掺入；超导转变温度2.2 K，动力学电感15 pH/sq，超导相干长度14.6 nm，与晶体相干长度相当。

Conclusion: 氢等离子体辅助ALD是获得结构有序超导TiN外延薄膜的有效途径，表明ALD的纳米级控制能力可拓展至适用于低温量子技术的材料体系。

Abstract: Atomic layer deposition (ALD) offers precise, conformal control of thin-film growth and is a workhorse for semiconductor manufacturing, but its use in superconducting quantum circuits is constrained by the need to simultaneously control crystallinity, stoichiometry and interfacial disorder at cryogenic temperatures. Here we develop hydrogen plasma assisted ALD to epitaxially grow superconducting titanium nitride (TiN) thin films, using a hydrogen plasma step to modify the surface chemistry to suppress precursor-derived impurities during each deposition cycle. Synchrotron X-ray scattering reveals semi-coherent epitaxy of TiN on c-plane sapphire, with discrete crystallographic domains and minimal long-range structural disorder. Complementary X-ray spectroscopy and neutron reflectometry show negligible oxygen-related disorder throughout the film and no substantial hydrogen incorporation. The resulting TiN films exhibit a superconducting transition at 2.2 K, a kinetic inductance of 15 pH/sq and a superconducting coherence length of 14.6 nm, comparable to the measured crystal coherence length. These results establish hydrogen plasma assisted ALD as a route to structurally ordered superconducting TiN thin film epitaxy and demonstrate that the nanoscale control of ALD can be extended to materials suitable for cryogenic quantum technologies.

</details>


### [153] [Charge and spin dynamics in spintronic THz emitters from Time-Dependent Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01089)
*Ali Kefayati*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用含时密度泛函理论，实时、实空间地揭示了Co/Pt与Co/W双层结构中自旋输运与电荷再分布并非唯一对应，而由材料特异的电子相空间决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋电子太赫兹发射器（铁磁/重金属异质结）虽能提供高效宽带太赫兹源，但超快自旋输运与电荷再分布之间的微观关联尚不清楚，亟需在原子尺度上揭示其耦合动力学机制。

Method: 采用含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT），对Co/Pt与Co/W双层结构的电荷、自旋与磁化动力学进行实时、实空间分辨，并分析自旋与轨道分辨的相空间、自旋-轨道介导的再分布及光诱导的格点间自旋转移。

Result: 尽管两体系的层间自旋流动力学定性相似，但层间电荷转移显著不同：Co/Pt表现为Pt→Co的较小且部分反向转移，而Co/W表现为Co→W的较大且持续转移；差异源于基态电子结构与光激发载流子可及的自旋、轨道分辨相空间。两体系均存在重金属向Co的延迟少子自旋转移；层间有净电子自旋角动量转移但无净轨道角动量转移；Co/W中电子密度由间隙态向原子中心态持续再分布，表明光诱导电子局域化。

Conclusion: 超快自旋输运并不能唯一决定自旋电子太赫兹发射器中的电荷与角动量再分布，后者关键依赖于材料特异的电子相空间，为异质结自旋电子太赫兹源的材料设计与微观理解提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Spintronic terahertz (THz) emitters based on ferromagnet/heavy-metal heterostructures provide efficient broadband sources of THz radiation, yet the microscopic relation between ultrafast spin transport and charge redistribution remains incompletely understood. Here, we employ time-dependent density functional theory to resolve the coupled charge, spin, and magnetization dynamics of Co/Pt and Co/W bilayers in real time and space. Despite qualitatively similar interlayer spin-current dynamics, the two heterostructures exhibit markedly different interlayer charge transfer: Co/Pt shows a relatively small and partially reversed transfer from Pt to Co, whereas Co/W exhibits a larger and persistent transfer from Co to W. We trace these differences to the material-dependent ground-state electronic structure and the spin- and orbital-resolved phase space accessible to photoexcited carriers. Spin-resolved dynamics further reveal coexisting spin-orbit-mediated redistribution and optically induced intersite spin transfer, including a delayed minority-spin transfer from the heavy metal to Co common to both systems. While net electronic spin angular momentum is transferred between the layers, we find no corresponding net transfer of electronic orbital angular momentum. In Co/W, the persistent redistribution of electronic density from interstitial to atom-centered states additionally provides strong evidence for light-induced electronic localization. These results demonstrate that ultrafast spin transport does not uniquely determine charge or angular-momentum redistribution in spintronic THz emitters, which instead depends critically on the material-specific electronic phase space.

</details>


### [154] [Gate Dispersive Charge Detection in Ge/SiGe Quantum Dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01100)
*M. R. Tanvir,E. Sajadi,M. Veldhorst,G. Scappucci,J. Salfi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出用于Ge/SiGe异质结量子点的紧凑RF栅极色散电荷读出方案，并借助单空穴盒将互点信号灵敏度显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ge异质结中的横向栅控量子点是自旋量子比特的重要实现平台，但现有RF单空穴晶体管与集成超导谐振器读出方案尺寸偏大，不利于扩展，因此需要更紧凑的读出手段。

Method: 在Ge双量子点（DQD）及单空穴盒（SHB）-DQD体系上实现紧凑的射频栅极色散电荷读出，通过栅极色散检测分辨量子点填充与互点跃迁，并进行功率依赖与温度依赖测量以分析噪声机制。

Result: 两种方法均能分辨量子点填充与互点跃迁；对于耦合tc≈5 GHz的DQD，SHB使540 MHz电路的互点信噪比提升20倍，135微秒达到SNR=1，而直接探测需2.7毫秒。功率与温度依赖测量表明SNR受介电损耗限制，其影响阻抗匹配。

Conclusion: 结果确立了面向Ge/SiGe异质结量子点的基于栅极色散检测的紧凑读出途径。

Abstract: Spin qubits are an attractive platform for scalable quantum information processing, and recently, laterally gated quantum dots (QDs) in Ge heterostructures have emerged as a leading implementation of spin qubits. To date, RF single-hole transistors (SHT) and integrated superconducting resonators have been used for spin and charge readout, but their size can pose challenges. Here we demonstrate compact RF gate-dispersive charge readout of a Ge double QD (DQD), and a single hole box (SHB)-DQD system. We resolve QD loading and interdot transitions with both techniques. We find that the SHB enhances the interdot signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of our 540 MHz circuit by a factor of 20 for a DQD coupling $t_c \approx 5$ GHz, providing a unity-SNR at 135 microseconds, c.f. for 2.7 miliseconds for the direct detection. Power- and temperature-dependent measurements verify that SNR is limited by dielectric loss which compromises impedance matching. These results establish a compact readout approach based on gate-dispersive detection for Ge/SiGe heterostructure QDs.

</details>


### [155] [Nutational Spin Pumping and Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01164)
*Hans Gløckner Giil,Arne Brataas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文揭示太赫兹驱动下磁化动力学的惯性区中存在“章动阻尼力矩”，包含体项与界面项，界面项导致“章动自旋泵浦”：直流自旋流随驱动频率二次方增长。作者用散射方法将其与微观材料参数联系，预测超薄膜与低费米能导体中效应显著增强，并给出逆自旋霍尔效应与厚度依赖阻尼重整化的探测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG) 理论无法描述太赫兹驱动频率下磁化动力学进入的惯性区，需要建立超越LLG的阻尼机制框架，并阐明其界面自旋输运后果。

Method: 理论分析：引入包含体项和界面贡献的章动阻尼力矩；采用散射方法（scattering approach）将界面项对应的自旋泵浦效应与微观材料参数建立联系。

Result: 界面项产生章动自旋泵浦——一种与驱动频率二次方成正比的直流自旋流；效应在超薄膜和低费米能导体中显著增强；可通过逆自旋霍尔效应直接探测，或通过厚度依赖的章动阻尼重整化间接探测。

Conclusion: 在太赫兹频段，惯性磁化动力学带来区别于传统LLG的章动阻尼与自旋泵浦新机制，为高频自旋电子学中的自旋流产生与探测提供了可验证的理论预测。

Abstract: At terahertz drive frequencies, magnetization dynamics enter an inertial regime beyond the scope of standard Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG) theory. We reveal a nutational damping torque comprising a bulk term and an interfacial contribution that describes spin emission into adjacent metals. The interfacial term generates nutational spin pumping: a dc spin current that scales quadratically with the drive frequency. Using a scattering approach, we relate this effect to microscopic material parameters and predict pronounced enhancements in ultrathin films and low-Fermi-energy conductors. Nutational spin pumping can be detected directly via the inverse spin Hall effect or indirectly via thickness-dependent renormalization of nutational damping.

</details>


### [156] [Synthesis of Metal-Semiconductor Heterojunctions in Single Graphene Nanoribbons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01194)
*Ziyi Wang,Kaitlin Slicker,Weichen Tang,Boyu Qie,Rafal Zuzak,Haowen Pang,Yudi Huang,Xinheng Li,Peter H. Jacobse,Steven G. Louie,Felix R. Fischer,Michael F. Crommie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过顺序表面合成与STM诱导脱氢，在保持宽度和碳骨架不变的前提下，将半导体H₂-7-iGNR局部转化为金属性7-iGNR段，形成原子级精确的金属-半导体GNR异质界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNR是高度可调的一维量子材料，带隙与带边可精确调控，是前景广阔的纳米电子平台，但构建高质量电子界面（尤其是金属-半导体接触）仍具挑战。

Method: 结合顺序表面合成与扫描隧道显微镜（STM）诱导脱氢，直接在半导体H₂-7-iGNR中写入金属性7-iGNR段；利用STS与第一性原理计算表征电子结构，并用扩展的Su-Schrieffer-Heeger锯齿梯子哈密顿量建模。

Result: 金属段在费米能级处呈现非零谱权重和空间延展的电子态，符合两条穿越EF的色散带；相邻半导体段价带边接近EF；STS显示界面两侧约1 nm内即恢复体相GNR性质，无明显耗尽区。

Conclusion: 观测到的带边对齐对应较小的空穴注入势垒，为未来GNR基电子器件实现原子级精确、低势垒的p型接触提供了可能路径。

Abstract: Graphene nanoribbons (GNRs) are a highly tunable class of one-dimensional (1D) quantum materials that can be fabricated through bottom-up synthesis. Precise control over GNR band gaps and band-edge alignments has established them as a promising nanoelectronics platform, but forming high-quality electronic interfaces remains challenging. Here we combine sequential on-surface synthesis and scanning tunneling microscopy (STM)-induced dehydrogenation to directly write metallic 7-iGNR segments into otherwise semiconducting H$_2$-7-iGNRs without changing the GNR width or carbon backbone connectivity. The resulting metallic segments exhibit nonzero spectral weight at the Fermi level ($E_\mathrm{F}$) and spatially extended electronic states, consistent with two dispersive bands crossing $E_\mathrm{F}$. The new metallic states are well described by an extended Su--Schrieffer--Heeger zigzag-ladder Hamiltonian. Scanning tunnelling spectroscopy (STS) and first-principles calculations show that the valence band edge of adjacent, connected semiconducting GNR segments lies close to $E_\mathrm{F}$ for the metallic segments. STS spectra recover bulk GNR properties within $\sim$1 nm on either side of the metal-semiconductor GNR interface, with no discernible depletion region. The observed GNR band edge alignment is consistent with a small energy barrier for hole injection, thus suggesting a possible route towards atomically precise, low-barrier p-type contacts for future GNR-based electronic devices.

</details>


### [157] [Phonon interference induced by defect pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01200)
*Ran-Bo Yang,Ming-Hao Li,Chun-Yu Cai,Yue-Wen Wang,Zhi-Qing Li,Li-Xia Zhao,Zi-Wu Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出缺陷对诱导声子干涉的理论模型，表明干涉条纹强度可由Huang-Rhys因子定量表征，几何构型可由缺陷对内禀参数调控，并可出现Dirac型与节点线拓扑态；结合Huang-Rhys模型模拟超快光谱，光谱强度与干涉条纹高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非平衡条件下电子-声子耦合的非绝热现象，需要理解晶格波（声子）的相干动力学；缺陷作为强局域电子-声子耦合的载体，可作为研究多自由度相干过程的平台。

Method: 构建由缺陷对诱导声子干涉的理论模型：两个缺陷因强局域电子-声子耦合各自独立发射选择性模式的晶格波，二者相干叠加形成干涉；以Huang-Rhys因子作为可测量量评估干涉条纹强度，并通过改变缺陷对的内禀参数（电荷态、空间距离、能级深度、局域化强度）调控干涉图样几何；进而基于Huang-Rhys模型模拟缺陷对作为色心被激发时的超快光学光谱。

Result: 声子干涉条纹强度可由Huang-Rhys因子直接评估；干涉图样几何随缺陷对内禀参数可调；在适当的内部参数下出现Dirac型与节点线（nodal-line）拓扑态等新奇量子态；超快光谱强度与声子干涉条纹完美匹配，呈现“锁-钥”（lock-key）式对应关系。

Conclusion: 缺陷对可作为多自由度相干动力学过程的驱动力，为在非平衡与超快物理中探索基本量子现象提供理想平台。

Abstract: The coherent dynamics of lattice waves is of significant importance for exploring the nonadiabatic phenomena of the electron-phonon coupling under nonequilibrium conditions. Here, we propose a theoretical model for phonon interference induced by a defect pair, in which lattice waves with the selective modes are launched independently by each of defects due to the strongly local electron-phonon coupling. We find that the intensities of phonon interference fringes could be directly evaluated by a measurable quantity in experiments--Huang-Rhys factor, and the geometry of interference patterns could be modified by the internal parameters of defect pair, including the charge states, spatial distance, energy-level depth and localization strength. In particular, several novel quantum states, such as the Dirac-like and nodal-line topological states are emerged for the appropriate internal parameters. Furthermore, using Huang-Rhys model, we simulate the ultrafast optical spectroscopy in the phonon interference duration when defect pairs are excited as the color centers, where interference fringes are perfectly matched by intensities of spectrum, similar to the $\textit{lock-key}$ pattern. These results show that defect pair, serving as the driven force for the coherent dynamical processes for multidegrees of freedom, offers an ideal platform to explore fundamental quantum phenomena in nonequilibrium and ultrafast physics.

</details>


### [158] [Composite fermions in ideal Chern bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01217)
*Songyang Pu,Liangtao Peng,Shaffique Adam*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将复合费米子框架推广至分数陈绝缘体，在Aharonov-Casher模型中构造Jain态及其准粒子波函数，发现其与精确本征态高度重合，并揭示涌现复合费米子能带结构超越传统有效朗道能级图像，孤立准粒子能带携带不同于朗道能级的总贝里通量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合费米子框架成功将强关联分数量子霍尔态映射为约化有效磁场中的弱相互作用整数量子霍尔态。然而在分数陈绝缘体中，均匀磁场的作用被陈能带的非均匀贝里曲率所取代，该框架如何被修正尚不清楚，亟需建立适用于可移动分数陈绝缘体任意子的复合费米子描述并理解其稳定性。

Method: 在Aharonov-Casher能带（一类广泛使用的理想陈能带模型）中构造Jain态及其准粒子和准空穴的复合费米子波函数，并将其与精确本征态进行对比。

Result: 所构造的复合费米子态在较宽的场调制范围内与精确本征态高度重合；涌现的复合费米子能带结构超越了传统有效朗道能级图像，能够正确刻画准粒子色散；一个孤立准粒子能带携带的总贝里通量不同于朗道能级。

Conclusion: 该工作为可移动分数陈绝缘体任意子建立了复合费米子描述，并提供了一个理解其稳定性的框架。

Abstract: The composite-fermion framework maps the strongly correlated fractional quantum Hall state onto a weakly interacting integer quantum Hall state in a reduced effective magnetic field. We ask how this framework is modified for fractional Chern insulators, where the role of a uniform magnetic field is replaced by the nonuniform Berry curvature of a Chern band. Specifically, we construct composite-fermion wave functions for Jain states and their quasiparticles and quasiholes in Aharonov-Casher bands, a widely used model for ideal Chern bands. We find that these states overlap closely with exact eigenstates over a broad range of field modulation. Using these wave functions, we demonstrate that the emergent composite-fermion band structure goes beyond the conventional picture of effective Landau levels, correctly capturing the quasiparticle dispersion. Remarkably, we find that an isolated quasiparticle band carries a total Berry flux that is different from a Landau level. These results establish a composite-fermion description for mobile fractional Chern insulator anyons and provide a framework to understand their stability.

</details>


### [159] [Inertial Dynamics of a Skymeron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01339)
*Mona Bhukta,Duc Minh Tran,Kilian Leutner,Takaaki Dohi,Nikolai S. Kiselev,Filipp N. Rybakov,Olle Eriksson,Fabian Kammerbauer,Sebastian Wintz,Markus Weigand,Hendrik Ohldag,Maria-Andromachi Syskaki,Robin Tietgen,Edoardo Mangini,Sabrina Kerber,Thibaud Denneulin,Joseph Vimal Vas,Rafal E. Dunin-Borkowski,Bastian Pfau,Oleg A. Tretiakov,Robert Frömter,Mathias Kläui*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过调控合成反铁磁双层间的有效磁各向异性差异，诱导自旋翻转转变，使反铁磁斯格明子重构为一种新型三维自旋织构"skymeron"（一层为面外斯格明子，另一层为面内bimeron耦合），并揭示其极性依赖的惯性式动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁斯格明子因补偿磁序而具有抑制斯格明子霍尔效应、超快动力学等优势，适合低功耗自旋电子器件。但本征反铁磁的两个子晶格完全相同，缺乏额外调控自由度；合成反铁磁允许独立调节两个铁磁层的性质，从而提供类似亚铁磁体的总磁矩调节与更多控制手段。本文旨在利用层选择性各向异性工程，探索传统相同子晶格反铁磁中无法实现的复合自旋织构及其内禀动力学自由度。

Method: 调节两个铁磁层之间的有效磁各向异性差异，驱动自旋翻转转变，使其中一层重新取向，稳定为"一层面外、另一层面内"的正交构型。利用元素分辨X射线显微术对织构进行逐层成像；采用时间分辨泵浦-探测X射线显微术观测电流脉冲下的动力学；并结合微磁学模拟分析其物理起源。

Result: 在自旋翻转转变中，反铁磁斯格明子发生同伦重构，形成一种复合自旋织构：一层承载面外斯格明子，另一层承载面内bimeron，二者耦合。元素分辨X射线显微术逐层证实了该结构（命名为skymeron）。时间分辨泵浦-探测测量发现极性依赖的独特动力学：脉冲后存在类似惯性的短暂持续运动，以及向钉扎初始态较慢的回复。微磁学模拟表明，这种惯性式传播源于skymeron重取向的有限时间弛豫。

Conclusion: 层选择性各向异性工程为构筑具有内部动力学自由度的新型复合自旋织构提供了途径，这类织构在子晶格完全相同的传统反铁磁中无法实现。

Abstract: Topological spin textures in antiferromagnets inherit the compensated magnetic order of the host material, resulting for skyrmions in the suppression of the skyrmion Hall effect and enabling ultrafast dynamics that make them attractive for low-power spintronic devices. In intrinsic antiferromagnets, the two sublattices are identical, but synthetic antiferromagnets offer additional control mechanisms by enabling independent tuning of the properties of two ferromagnetic layers. Analogous to a ferrimagnet, the total magnetic moment can be adjusted by compensating the two-layer moments. Here, we demonstrate that tuning the effective magnetic anisotropy difference between the two layers drives a spin-flop transition where one layer reorients and thus stabilizes an orthogonal configuration with one layer oriented along the out-of-plane direction and the other in-plane. In this transition, an antiferromagnetic skyrmion undergoes a homotopic reconfiguration into a complex spin texture comprising a skyrmion coupled to an in-plane bimeron. Element-specific X-ray microscopy resolves this texture layer by layer: one hosts an out-of-plane skyrmion coupled to an in-plane bimeron in the other. We refer to this previously unexplored three-dimensional spin texture as a skymeron. By using time-resolved pump-probe X-ray microscopy, we discover unique polarity ,dependent dynamics during current pulses: a short post-pulse inertia-like continuation of the motion, and a slower return towards the pinned initial state. Micromagnetic simulations reveal that the inertia-like propagation originates from the finite-time relaxation of reorientation of the skymeron. Our results establish layer-selective anisotropy engineering as a route to uncharted composite spin textures with internal dynamical degrees of freedom, not possible in conventional antiferromagnets with identical sublattices.

</details>


### [160] [Transport theory for a generic two-arm co-propagating Majorana interferometer with Majorana fermion and edge vortex tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01402)
*Domenico Giuliano,Andrea Nava,Fabian Hassler,Reinhold Egger*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在近邻耦合的量子反常霍尔系统中，共传播手性Majorana边缘模式可通过一对Majorana边缘态的干涉实现全电学探测，其低频有效电容携带边缘涡旋的非阿贝尔任意子统计（拓扑自旋与共形维度）签名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共传播手性Majorana边缘模式有望将边缘涡旋作为"飞行"的非阿贝尔Ising任意子，并通过任意子融合过程实现全电学探测。但此前处理多限于对称构型，需要推广到完全非对称情形以刻画Mach-Zehnder干涉仪的输运响应。

Method: 对模型引入额外replica扇区进行玻色化，推广至完全非对称构型，并分析Mach-Zehnder干涉仪中的电输运。

Result: 直流电导不显示边缘涡旋干涉特征；但在低频下该干涉表现为有效电容，该电容对边缘涡旋的非阿贝尔任意子统计敏感，携带其拓扑自旋与非平凡共形维度的签名。低温柔化长度 $l_T = \hbar v/(\pi k_B T)$ 为相关尺度，与常规量子霍尔边缘态中准粒子输运类似。

Conclusion: 只要干涉仪两臂长度在该热长度尺度内匹配，边缘涡旋非阿贝尔统计的输运签名仍然可见。

Abstract: Co-propagating chiral Majorana edge modes are predicted to exist in proximity-coupled quantum anomalous Hall systems. They combine the exciting prospects of edge vortices as flying non-Abelian Ising anyons, with their fully electrical detection through anyon fusion processes. In this setup, electrical transport arises from the interference of a pair of Majorana edge states. By bosonizing the model with an additional replica sector, we generalize previous treatments to fully asymmetric configurations. We analyze a Mach-Zehnder interferometer and show that while the DC conductance does not exhibit signatures of edge-vortex interference, at low frequencies, this interference manifests as an effective capacitance. This quantity is sensitive to the non-Abelian anyon statistics of edge vortices and, in particular, carries signatures of their topological spin and nontrivial conformal dimension. We demonstrate that the thermal length $l_T = \hbar v/(πk_B T)$ is the relevant scale at low temperatures $T$, analogous to quasiparticle transport in conventional quantum Hall edge states with velocity $v$. As long as the interferometer arm lengths match within this length scale, transport signatures of the non-Abelian statistics of edge vortices remain visible.

</details>


### [161] [Sector-Resolved Winding Selection Rules for Structured-Light-Driven dc Currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01423)
*Tomohiro Tamaya,Kenichi L. Ishikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用石墨烯作为平台，通过将电流响应按算子层面分解为局域与梯度扇区，揭示了结构光驱动直流电流方位角缠绕序的选择定则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构光可产生具有方位角缠绕的直流电流，但其缠绕序 m 的支配规律尚不清晰，亟需明确的选择定则。

Method: 在当前算符层面将电流响应分解为局域扇区和梯度扇区，分析相应电流算符的角度结构与向方位角方向的投影，并结合数值时间演化计算验证。

Result: 缠绕序不由光学轨道角动量 ℓ 单独决定。对标量拉盖尔-高斯光束：线偏振时局域扇区给出 m=|ℓ±1|，梯度扇区给出 m=|ℓ|,|ℓ±2|；圆偏振（螺旋度 σ=±1）时局域扇区选择 m=|ℓ-σ|，梯度扇区选择 m=|ℓ+2σ|。数值计算验证了这些定则。

Conclusion: 螺旋度可选出 m=0 分支，产生方位角均匀的环流，其径向分布决定轴向磁场 B_z(z)；结果为结构光驱动直流电流的分类与调控提供了分扇区的组织原则。

Abstract: Structured light can generate electronic dc currents with azimuthal winding, yet the rules governing their winding order $m$ remain unclear. Here we identify sector-resolved winding selection rules using graphene as a clean two-dimensional platform. By decomposing the current response into local and gradient sectors at the current-operator level, we show that the winding order is determined not by the optical orbital angular momentum $\ell$ alone, but by the angular structure of the corresponding current operators together with projection onto the azimuthal direction. For scalar Laguerre-Gaussian beams, linear polarization yields $m=|\ell\pm1|$ in the local sector and $m=|\ell|,|\ell\pm2|$ in the gradient sector, whereas circular polarization with helicity $σ=\pm1$ selects $m=|\ell-σ|$ in the local sector and $m=|\ell+2σ|$ in the gradient sector. Numerical time-evolution calculations verify these rules and further show that helicity can select an $m=0$ branch, producing an azimuthally uniform circulating current whose radial profile determines the axial magnetic field $B_z(z)$. Our results provide a sector-resolved organizing principle for classifying and controlling structured-light-driven dc currents with tailored winding structures.

</details>


### [162] [Parafermions in fractional Chern insulator-superconductor heterostructures: the role of spin polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01486)
*Aaron Amire*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文分析了自旋-谷极化 ν=2/3 边缘（FQAH 体系）构成沟道中的 Z3 仲费米子（parafermion）实现，指出配对与隧穿分别只允许中性模超导与中性模背散射两个通道，二者凝聚同一中性任意子，故畴壁携带 Z3 仲费米子，每个配对有 3 个受保护态，并给出相关耦合的标度维度与超导配对对称性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Z3 仲费米子方案多基于自旋非极化 ν=2/3 Halperin (1,1,2) 态，而扭转 MoTe2 与菱面体石墨烯的 FQAH 态被认为是自旋-谷极化的 Jain 态，拓扑序相同但自旋结构不同；需要厘清该差异对边缘沟道中任意子凝聚与受保护简并的影响。

Method: 对两条极化 ν=2/3 边缘间的沟道进行分析，考察边缘模电子双线性算子的共形自旋；区分中性模超导配对与中性模背散射隧穿通道，比较极化与非极化情形；通过耦合相关性的标度维度分析配对与电荷项的（边缘）相关性，并核算畴壁任意子凝聚所导致的简并度及费米子宇称固定效应。

Result: 两种双线性中各恰有两个共形自旋为零；配对仅与中性模超导通道相容，隧穿仅与中性模背散射相容；非极化态中单重态配对与隧穿则选择同一通道。由于两通道（至多差一局域算符）凝聚相同中性任意子，畴壁携带 Z3 仲费米子，每对 3 个受保护态，无论中性通道是否在区域间改变，固定费米子宇称也不增加计数。中性能隙形成前，对排斥性边缘间耦合配对最多是边缘性（marginal），除非吸引的中性耦合偏向其通道；能隙形成后，当边缘间库仑耦合小于边缘内耦合的 7/25 时，两电荷项均为相关（relevant）。超导体须提供偶频、等自旋、谷内配对：仅有 Ising 自旋轨道耦合的 s 波单重态超导体在领头阶无法提供，而手性 p+ip 与 f+if 态可以。

Conclusion: Z3 标签由 FQAH 态的准粒子携带，故任何局限于隧穿段非 FQAH 的结的物理过程都不能改变它；预期无论是否固定费米子宇称，都存在 6π 周期的 Josephson 电流。

Abstract: Most proposals for $Z_3$ parafermions in fractional quantum Hall-superconductor structures used the spin-unpolarized $ν= 2/3$ Halperin $(1,1,2)$ state. The fractional quantum anomalous Hall (FQAH) states of twisted MoTe$_2$ and rhombohedral graphene are believed to be spin- and valley-polarized Jain states, with the same topological order but a different spin structure. We analyze a trench between two polarized $ν= 2/3$ edges. Among the pairing and tunneling bilinears of the edge-mode electrons, exactly two of each have zero conformal spin. Pairing is compatible only with the neutral-mode superconductivity channel and tunneling only with neutral-mode backscattering, whereas in the unpolarized state singlet pairing and tunneling select the same channel. Since the two channels condense the same neutral anyon up to a local operator, the domain walls carry $Z_3$ parafermions, with 3 protected states per pair, whether or not the neutral channel changes between regions; fixing the fermion parity does not enlarge this count. Before the neutral gap forms, pairing is at best marginal for repulsive inter-edge coupling unless an attractive neutral coupling favors its channel; once the gap has formed, both charge terms are relevant for an inter-edge Coulomb coupling below 7/25 of the intra-edge one. The superconductor must supply even-frequency, equal-spin, intravalley pairing, which an s-wave singlet superconductor with only Ising spin-orbit coupling does not provide at leading order, whereas chiral $p+ip$ and $f+if$ states do. The $Z_3$ label is carried by a quasiparticle of the FQAH state, so no process confined to a junction whose tunneling segments are not FQAH can change it, and we expect a $6π$-periodic Josephson current with or without fixed fermion parity.

</details>


### [163] [Single-Pulse Optical Switching combined with Current-Induced Motion of Skyrmionic Spin Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01568)
*Edoardo Mangini,Duc Minh Tran,Boonthum Kunyangyuen,Jun Xiao Lin,Raphael Gruber,Michel Hehn,Stéphane Mangin,Mathias Kläui*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在全光学、无 helicity 依赖的磁化翻转（AO-HIS）与磁性 skyrmion 之间建立耦合，实现用单发超快激光脉冲确定性翻转 skyrmion 的自旋结构（极性），并与电流驱动的 skyrmion 运动结合，为光-自旋电子学 skyrmion 器件提供方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性 skyrmion 作为纳米尺度信息载体，其位置可由电流操控，但若能确定性控制其自旋结构，则可作为多态比特，扩展器件功能。已有报道的光学操控仅限于 skyrmion 的成核与湮灭，缺乏对其自旋结构的确定性调控通道。

Method: 设计 Pt/Ir/CoB/Gd/Pt 多层膜，使其既能稳定磁性 skyrmion，又能实现 AO-HIS。用激光激发写入 skyrmion 织构，其极性由小的垂直外加磁场选择；再用单发 30 fs 激光脉冲翻转含 skyrmion 织构区域的磁化，从而反转其极性，无需磁场或电流脉冲。随后将材料图形化为线状结构，将光学操控与电流驱动的 skyrmion 运动相结合。

Result: 在无磁场、无电流脉冲条件下，单发 30 fs 激光脉冲实现了对含 skyrmion 织构区域磁化的翻转，进而反转其极性；实现了对 skyrmion 自旋结构的确定性光学开关，区别于此前报道的光学成核与湮灭。在图形化的线状结构中，实现了光学操控与电流驱动 skyrmion 运动的结合。

Conclusion: 确立了确定性光学开关 skyrmion 自旋结构这一新的操控通道，并展示了与电流驱动运动协同的可能，指向一种光-自旋电子学 skyrmion 器件路线：由光编程 skyrmion 的内部状态，由电流控制其位置。

Abstract: Magnetic skyrmions are promising nanoscale information carriers because their position can be manipulated by electrical currents. The additional ability of deterministic control of the spin structure would allow the use of magnetic skyrmions as multi-state bits, expanding the capabilities of skyrmion devices further. Such control could be realized by coupling all-optical helicity independent switching of magnetization (AO-HIS) to magnetic skyrmions. This is realized in a Pt/Ir/CoB/Gd/Pt multilayer that is engineered to stabilize magnetic skyrmions and, at the same time, enable AO-HIS by single ultrafast laser pulse. Laser excitation is used to write skyrmionic textures, whose polarity is selected by a small applied out-of-plane field. A single 30 fs laser pulse is then used to toggle the magnetization of an illuminated region containing skyrmionic textures, reversing their polarities without requiring any magnetic field or current pulses. This establishes deterministic optical switching of the skyrmion spin structure as a new manipulation channel, distinct from previously reported optical nucleation and annihilation. The material is then patterned into a wire, to combine the optical manipulation with current-induced skyrmion motion. These results establish a route toward opto-spintronic skyrmion devices in which light programs the internal state of a skyrmion and electrical currents control its position.

</details>


### [164] [Flux- and tunnel-parity-controlled Josephson nonreciprocity from singlet-triplet competition in a parallel double quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01830)
*Debika Debnath,Fernando Dominguez,Patrik Recher*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究平行双量子点Josephson结（强库仑排斥）中的非互易超流机制，发现轨道磁通与规范不变的隧穿符号奇偶性控制局域与非局域过程干涉，产生可调磁通的单重态、三重态与双重态分支及两种整流区，二极管效率可达~40%（电荷交叉边界）及10-15%（三重态区）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易超流（Josephson二极管效应）通常源于正负临界电流探测相位依赖多体谱的不等价部分。作者希望理解相互作用纳米结构中"多体分支竞争"这一机制，尤其是在局域与非局域库珀对转移共存的平行双量子点体系中，如何通过栅压、磁通和隧穿奇偶性调控非互易性。

Method: 采用零带宽哈密顿量的精确对角化（exact diagonalization），显式包含超导轨道，对具有强位内库仑排斥的平行双量子点Josephson结建模，分析轨道磁通与规范不变隧穿符号奇偶性对局域Josephson过程与非局域交换之间干涉的调控作用，并考察失谐量子点情形下的基态谱分支与有限温度效应。

Result: 在失谐量子点情形下，出现磁通可调的单重态、三重态与双重态基态谱分支及两种整流区。在(1,1)电荷扇区边界附近，双重态分支与单重态或三重态分支竞争，二极管效率接近40%。在三重态主导的(1,1)扇区内，邻近单重态交叉非对称重塑基态包络，即使两个临界电流极值都位于类三重态分支上，也产生约10-15%的较宽响应。有限温度通常抑制电荷交叉整流，但在中间温度下三重态区的归一化效率与绝对临界电流不对称性均增强。

Conclusion: 相位依赖的多体分支竞争是Josephson非互易性的一个对栅压、磁通和隧穿奇偶性敏感的起源，为设计和调控Josephson二极管提供了新机制。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal supercurrents in interacting nanostructures can arise when the positive and negative critical currents probe inequivalent portions of a phase-dependent many-body spectrum. We study this mechanism in a parallel double-quantum-dot Josephson junction with strong intradot Coulomb repulsion, where local and non-local Cooper-pair transfer coexist. Using exact diagonalization of a zero-bandwidth Hamiltonian with explicit superconducting orbitals, we show that orbital flux and the gauge-invariant tunnel-sign parity control the interference between local Josephson processes and non-local exchange. For detuned dots, this produces flux-tunable singlet, triplet, and doublet ground-state spectral branches and two rectification regimes. Near the boundaries of the charge sector with singly occupied dots [(1,1) sector], doublet branches compete with singlet or triplet branches, yielding diode efficiencies approaching 40%. Within the triplet-dominated (1,1) sector, nearby singlet crossings asymmetrically reshape the ground-state envelope even when both critical-current extrema lie on the triplet-like branch, producing a broader response of order 10-15%. Finite temperature generally suppresses charge-crossover rectification, however, for intermediate temperatures we find an enhancement of both the normalized efficiency and the absolute critical-current asymmetry in the triplet regime. These results establish phase-dependent many-body branch competition as a gate-, flux-, and tunnel-parity-sensitive source of Josephson nonreciprocity.

</details>


### [165] [Symmetry considerations in chirality-induced spin selectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01880)
*Dmitry Budker,Angela Wittmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文是一篇观点文章，指出手性诱导自旋选择性（CISS）效应可借助基本粒子物理中常用的对称性分析来理解，且CISS并不违反宇称与时间反演等基本对称性，从而为实验解释与理论设计提供统一的对称性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CISS效应已在多种体系中实验观测到，即结构手性与电子自旋极化之间的耦合，但长期缺乏统一的理论机制理解，阻碍了对实验的解释与后续理论与实验设计。

Method: 采用基础粒子物理中常用的对称性分析（parity、time-reversal等）对CISS的基本性质进行直接的对称性论证，而非建立具体微观模型。

Result: 表明CISS并不违反包括宇称与时间反演在内的任何基本对称性，其基本性质可由对称性考量加以理解。

Conclusion: 通过将CISS置于普适的对称性语言中，为解释相关实验以及指引未来理论与实验设计提供了稳健的概念基础。

Abstract: The chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS) effect, the coupling between structural chirality and electron spin polarization, has been experimentally observed across many diverse systems. However, despite extensive theoretical effort, a unified mechanistic understanding remains elusive. In this perspective, we demonstrate how some of the basic properties of the fascinating effects of CISS can be understood based on straightforward symmetry considerations commonly employed in fundamental particle physics. In particular, we show that CISS does not violate any fundamental symmetries including parity and time-reversal. By anchoring CISS within the universal language of symmetry, we offer a robust conceptual foundation for interpreting experiments and guiding future theoretical and experimental designs.

</details>
